Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAL和SEAL++，一种基于共享专家（shared expert）的防御方法，通过在MoE模型中利用共享专家中的安全关键神经元作为路由器无关锚点，以参数高效的方式增强模型的安全对齐，抵抗对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE架构存在结构性安全漏洞：模型安全性依赖于被激活的专家子集，攻击者可通过越狱提示、恶意微调和神经元剪枝操纵或绕过路由选择。现有防御主要强化路由器，但路由过程的不确定性使攻击者仍可操纵路由轨迹，导致防御失效。

Method: 首先从理论和实证上验证共享专家（始终激活的组件）中少量安全关键神经元可克服稀疏路由路径的不确定性，作为路由器无关的安全锚点。基于此提出SEAL（训练时参数高效防御，生成可插拔适配器附加到共享专家）和SEAL++（SEAL的变体，添加正交约束以在训练中保留既有安全子空间）。

Result: 在六种攻击场景（三种对抗输入×有无神经元剪枝）中评估，SEAL将攻击成功率（ASR）降低最多60%，能力损失在五个基准平均值上最多1.4%。SEAL还可与路由器级防御无缝集成。

Conclusion: 共享专家可作为路由器无关的安全锚点，SEAL和SEAL++提供了一种有效的训练时参数高效防御方案，兼顾安全性与模型能力，且可与现有路由器级防御互补集成。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a scaling architecture for large language models that activates only a small subset of expert modules per token, enabling massive parameter growth with nearly constant computation. Recent Hybrid MoE architecture adds \textit{shared experts} to capture consistently useful representations, further improving stability and generalization. MoE now powers many flagship open-source and commercial models, yet remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Specifically, sparse routing introduces a structural vulnerability: MoE safety hinges on which experts are activated, and adversaries can subvert this selection through jailbreak prompts, malicious fine-tuning, and weight-level pruning of safety-critical neurons. Existing defenses primarily focus on hardening the router, but an adversary may still manipulate or bypass the routing trajectory due to the routing process's nondeterministic nature, thereby collapsing the defense. To cope with this problem, we first identify theoretically and empirically that shared expert, an always-activated component containing a small proportion of safety-critical neurons, can overcome the uncertainty of sparsely activated routing path and serve as a router-independent anchor to enhance global safety alignment. Based on this insight, we propose SEAL, a training-time parameter-efficient defense that produces a plug-and-play adapter attached to shared expert, and SEAL++, a variant that adds an orthogonal constraint preserving pre-existing safety subspaces during training. We evaluate SEAL and SEAL++ across six attack scenarios that combine three adversarial inputs (harmful prompting, jailbreak, malicious fine-tuning) with and without neuron pruning. SEAL reduces attack success rate (ASR) by up to 60\%, at a capability cost of at most 1.4\% on a five-benchmark average. Additionally, SEAL can seamlessly integrate with router-level ......

</details>


### [52] [Bayes-Optimal BER and AUC: Estimation and Evaluation of Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02304)
*Ryota Ushio,Takashi Ishida,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种估计最优平衡错误率（BER）和ROC曲线下面积（AUC）的新方法，这两个指标在类别不平衡或噪声标注场景下比传统准确率更适用。作者设计了基于软标签的估计器，涵盖理想场景（已知真软标签和类别先验）及更实际的场景（类别先验未知、软标签受未知保序变换和加性噪声污染），并通过等渗回归与辅助硬标签进行近似恢复。同时，作者扩展了FeeBee框架用于评估这类估计器，无需知道真实最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中估计任何模型在给定任务上的最优性能至关重要，但现有工作仅针对二元分类中的最优准确率（贝叶斯误差）提出估计方法。然而在类别严重不平衡或标注噪声存在时，准确率不是好的性能度量，BER和AUC更为合适，但如何估计这两者的最优值仍是一个空白。

Method: (i) 估计方面：提出基于软标签的最优BER和AUC估计器。先在理想场景（真软标签和类别先验已知）下设计基础估计器，再扩展到更现实场景（类别先验未知，软标签被未知保序变换和加性噪声破坏），通过等渗回归配合辅助硬标签近似恢复干净软标签，用硬标签的裁剪均值估计类别先验，并推导插件估计器的有限样本误差界。(ii) 评估方面：将FeeBee框架从贝叶斯误差估计扩展到最优BER和AUC估计，无需知道最优值即可对任何此类估计器进行实用评估打分。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验验证了所提出的估计器和评估流程均有效。估计器在类不平衡和噪声标注场景下能准确估计最优BER和AUC，扩展的FeeBee评估框架也能在不知道真实最优值的情况下可靠评估不同估计器的表现。

Conclusion: 本文填补了在类不平衡和噪声标注场景下估计最优BER和AUC的空白，提出了具有有限样本保证的估计器，并提供了无需知道最优值即可评估此类估计器的实用框架，为区分模型不可约误差与模型缺陷提供了更合适的工具。

Abstract: A fundamental quantity in machine learning is the optimal performance achievable by any model on a given task. Estimating this quantity allows us to distinguish the irreducible part of the error from a deficiency of the model, telling us how much room for improvement remains. Recent work has shown that the Bayes error, or equivalently the optimal accuracy, can be estimated from soft labels in binary classification. However, accuracy is often a poor summary of performance in settings with severe class imbalance or noisy annotations, where metrics such as the balanced error rate (BER) and the area under the ROC curve (AUC) are more appropriate. We address this gap with two complementary contributions. (i) Estimation. We propose soft-label-based estimators for the optimal BER and AUC. We first consider the clean setting in which true soft labels and the class prior are known, and then extend the estimators to a more realistic setting in which the class prior is unknown and the observed soft labels are corrupted by an unknown order-preserving transformation, possibly followed by additive noise. In the latter setting, we approximately recover the clean soft labels via isotonic regression with auxiliary hard labels, estimate the class prior with a clipped mean of the hard labels, and derive finite-sample error bounds for the resulting plug-in estimators. (ii) Evaluation. Since the optimum is unobservable on real datasets, evaluating any such estimator is itself nontrivial. We extend the FeeBee framework, originally proposed for evaluating Bayes-error estimators, to the optimal BER and AUC. The resulting procedure provides practical evaluation scores without requiring knowledge of the optimum, and applies to any estimator of the optimal BER or AUC, not only our proposed ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate both the estimators and the evaluation procedure.

</details>


### [53] [What Is Worth Representing? Representational Empowerment for Continual Model Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02322)
*Fei Dai,Hanqi Zhou,Alison Gopnik,Charley Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征赋权（RepEmp）原则，用于解决持续模型构建中"应该表征什么"的核心问题——通过评估候选表征元素对未来建模和规划能力的扩展程度来决定构建、保留和复用什么。作者设计了Curator-Actor分层架构，在因果学习、模拟和规划三个实验中验证了RepEmp优于信息增益等替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模的第一性问题不是估计正确参数或因果结构，而是决定什么内容值得被表征。现有方法缺乏一个统一原则来指导代理在资源受限条件下如何进行持续模型构建和表征库管理。

Method: 提出RepEmp评分标准，将经典empowerment概念从外部状态控制重新定义为内部表征控制。实现为层级Curator-Actor架构，并通过三项实验验证：封闭词汇因果学习任务（人类实验）、匹配仿真实验、以及开放词汇规划领域（LLM增强Curator）。

Result: 三项实验结果共同表明：人类在因果建模中选择最大化目标可达性而非世界保真度的抽象粒度，这一行为特征能被RepEmp更好地预测；RepEmp引导的构建比纯探索对结构恢复和跨任务迁移贡献更大；LLM增强的Curator能构建更紧凑且泛化更好的符号库。消融RepEmp后这些优势消失。

Conclusion: RepEmp是持续模型构建的关键原则，在有限资源约束下指导代理决定构建什么、保留什么和复用什么样的表征元素，从而在建模能力与规划能力之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The first problem of modeling the world is not just estimating the right parameters or causal structure, but deciding what should be represented at all. We frame this problem as continual model construction: an agent maintains an environment-specific model M of an inaccessible world W and curates a persistent library L of reusable representational elements across environments. We propose Representational Empowerment (RepEmp) to score candidate elements by how much they expand the agent's future capacity to model and plan, complementing the classic definition of empowerment, but redefined as control over internal representations instead of external states. We realize the framework as a hierarchical Curator-Actor architecture and test it across three experiments. In a closed-vocabulary causal-learning task, human participants construct causal models at varying abstraction granularities to maximize goal reachability rather than fidelity to the world, a signature better predicted by RepEmp than by information-gain alternatives. Matched simulations reveal that RepEmp-guided construction contributes more than exploration to sufficient structure recovery and cross-task transfer. Finally, in an open-vocabulary planning domain, an LLM-augmented Curator builds more compact symbolic libraries, which also generalize better than baselines. Ablating RepEmp eliminates these benefits. Together, these results identify RepEmp as a key principle for continual model construction: deciding what to build, retain, and reuse under bounded resources.

</details>


### [54] [AGI Maze Prediction Datasets: A Compact Benchmark for Learning World Dynamics with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02339)
*Alexey Potapov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGI迷宫预测数据集与基准测试，用于研究Transformer等预测模型在世界建模中的能力。实验表明，结构化、任务对齐的工作记忆架构比仅增加隐容量更能提升 held-out 预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模需要预测模型维护和更新内部状态以推理行动后果，但现有基准缺乏轻量级、可控的测试环境来研究这一能力，特别是在结构化工作记忆与语言接地的关系方面。

Method: 从程序化生成的有状态网格世界中构建基准，包含逐步转移预测、固定视界状态预测和序列文本观测预测三类任务；采用源迷宫分离的训练/验证划分和贪心精确匹配评估；比较了从头训练的字节级Transformer基线、无结构辅助隐记忆Transformer和伪视频空间记忆Transformer。

Result: 通用隐记忆Transformer可完全拟合部分训练集但在验证集上无持续提升；伪视频空间记忆Transformer在选定固定视界任务上达到完美验证准确率（字节基线和隐记忆基线均未达到），并显著提升序列文本轨迹预测性能。

Conclusion: 结构化、任务对齐的工作记忆比单纯的额外隐容量更有用；语言接地的实现依赖于持久化数据结构及其上的计算，该基准为测试将文本接口与学习到的结构化状态耦合的架构提供了紧凑的实验环境。

Abstract: World modeling requires a predictive model to maintain and update an internal state adequate for reasoning about the consequences of actions. We introduce the AGI Maze Prediction Datasets and Benchmark, a lightweight controlled testbed for studying this capability in Transformers and other predictive models. Derived from procedurally generated, stateful grid worlds, the benchmark comprises per-step transition prediction, fixed-horizon state prediction, and sequential textual-observation prediction. Source-maze-disjoint training and validation splits, together with greedy exact-match evaluation, distinguish learning transferable action-conditioned dynamics from memorizing transitions in familiar layouts. We establish from-scratch byte-level Transformer baselines and compare them with two working-memory-augmented architectures. A generic auxiliary latent-memory Transformer can fit some training sets perfectly but does not consistently improve held-out performance. In contrast, a pseudo-video spatial-memory Transformer initializes a two-dimensional latent workspace from the input map and updates it from action history without receiving intermediate maps, positions, or state labels. Under the same data, objectives, and evaluation protocol, this model reaches perfect validation accuracy on selected fixed-horizon tasks where the byte and unstructured-memory baselines do not, and substantially improves sequential text-trace prediction. These results suggest that structured, task-aligned working memory can be more useful than additional latent capacity alone. More broadly, we argue that language grounding is mediated by persistent data structures and computations over them; the benchmark offers a compact setting for testing architectures that couple textual interfaces to learned structured state.

</details>


### [55] [Percolation Dynamics in Optimization : Variance Cascades and Discrete Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02373)
*Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机梯度下降（SGD）的动态过程，将其建模为渗透过程（percolation process），揭示了子网络在架构对称性作用下以离散同时块的形式合并的结构转变，并通过宏观序参量的方差尖峰来表征，类似于物理相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD已知会将深度神经网络引导向对应更简单子网络的不变集合，但这一引导过程随时间的演变机制尚不清楚，缺乏对其动态过程的深入理解。

Method: 将随机梯度流（SGF）建模为渗透过程，利用架构对称性迫使子网络以离散的同步块（而非逐一）方式合并；通过宏观序参量中的方差尖峰来追踪这些结构转变；并将该陷获机制及相关联的缩放级联扩展到Adam和AdamW优化器，显式建模重尾噪声。

Result: 结构转变以离散同步块的形式发生，并在宏观序参量中表现为方差尖峰，与物理相变现象呼应；该机制同样适用于Adam和AdamW在重尾噪声模型下的表现。

Conclusion: SGD引导网络走向更简单子网络的过程可以通过渗透理论来理解，且该陷获机制及其缩放级联具有跨优化器（SGD、Adam、AdamW）的普适性。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD), which is known to steer deep neural networks toward invariant sets that correspond to simpler subnetworks. How this steering unfolds over time remains poorly understood. We answer this by modeling the stochastic gradient flow (SGF) as a percolation process, in which architectural symmetries force subnetworks to merge in discrete simultaneous blocks rather than one at a time. These structural transitions register as variance spikes in a macroscopic order parameter, echoing physical phase transitions. We further show this trapping mechanism and its associated scaling cascade extend to Adam and AdamW under an explicit heavy-tailed noise model.

</details>


### [56] [Evidence for Shared Routing Geometry and Dynamics in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02404)
*Kirill Labzin,Stepan Kulibaba,Artem Dzhalilov,Artem Gorokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏MoE模型中跨层路由状态共享一个通用几何结构，通过广义正交Procrustes分析将对齐后的控制子空间统一到规范坐标系中，揭示路由状态演化遵循可复用的跨层线性动态过程，且该动态保留了路由特定信息而非仅仅反映隐藏表示的平滑演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究观察到稀疏MoE的路由决策可从前层的路由信号中预测，但跨层路由并非完全独立的结构性原因尚不清楚，作者希望揭示这种可预测性背后的共同几何结构。

Method: 隔离每层路由器的控制子空间，利用广义正交Procrustes分析将这些子空间对齐到共享的规范表示；经过对齐后拟合单一线性转移来刻画跨层路由状态演化，并通过与独立拟合的逐层动态、残差表示及随机基线进行对照实验验证方法的有效性。

Result: 对齐后的单一线性转移达到R²=0.39-0.71，保留逐层独立动态79-90%的预测力；匹配秩对照表明路由器控制状态比残差表示更能保真地保留模型的专家选择；使用预测的规范状态替代原生路由状态时，局部路由行为得以保留，学习到的状态演化相比简单持久性在OLMoE上ΔNLL降低15.7%，在Phi上10路由范围降低6.2%。

Conclusion: 路由状态的跨层演化遵循一个可复用的共享线性动态过程，该过程并非简单地反映通用隐藏表示的平滑演变，而是包含路由特有的决策信息，可作为有效的预测信号用于替代或引导实际路由过程。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) models use an independently parameterized router at each sparse layer to select experts for every token. Prior work has shown that routing decisions across depth can often be predicted from earlier routing signals, suggesting that routing is not fully independent across layers. However, the structure behind this predictability remains unclear. In this work, we provide evidence that routing-relevant states across layers share a common geometric structure that is obscured by layer-specific coordinate systems. We isolate the control subspace of each router and align these spaces into a shared canonical representation using generalized orthogonal Procrustes analysis. After alignment, a single linear transition reaches $R^2=0.39$--$0.71$ and retains 79--90\% of the predictive power of separately fitted layer-specific dynamics, indicating that much of routing-state evolution follows a reusable process across depth. We then ask whether this shared dynamics is specific to routing or simply reflects the smooth evolution of hidden representations. A matched-rank comparison shows that residual representations are often easier to predict across layers, while router-control states preserve the model's expert choices much more faithfully. This separates generic cross-layer predictability from routing-specific information. Finally, we test whether the predicted canonical states remain meaningful when used in place of native routing states. The transported states preserve local routing behavior, while learned state evolution reduces $Δ\mathrm{NLL}$ relative to simple persistence by 15.7\% on OLMoE and 6.2\% over a 10-router horizon on Phi.

</details>


### [57] [Coverage, Not Targeting: A Structural Regime in Multi-Turn Agent Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02417)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多轮智能体强化学习中的信用分配问题。作者提出验证器信息密度 V_d = k/C 作为预测"按轮次定位信用"是否有效的结构量。通过受控实验显示，在终端状态验证的低 V_d 场景下，均匀分布的稠密奖励优于稀疏二值奖励，而将奖励集中在特定轮次（进度轮或随机轮）反而有害，说明定位式信用分配是次优策略。实验在 tau²-bench、BFCL V3、ToolACE-2-8B 等多个基准上验证，并提出了匹配浓度混洗对照作为定位式方法必须通过的检验标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体RL常把信用分配当作定位问题——在给定的终端可验证奖励下，按轮次方法把信用定位到关键轮次。但作者想弄清：什么时候这种"定位"式方法是正确的？他们识别出一个关键的结构量（验证器信息密度 V_d）来预测该方法的适用边界，并质疑终端状态验证器场景下定位式方法的有效性。

Method: 提出验证器信息密度 V_d = k/C 作为理论框架。在受控共享回滚条件下进行实验对比：(1) 均匀分布的稠密连续奖励 vs 稀疏二值终端奖励；(2) 将稠密奖励集中在进度轮次 vs 随机轮次（targeting）。在 tau²-bench、BFCL V3、ToolACE-2-8B 上开展32个预注册种子的跨模型族实验，并设计了匹配浓度混洗（matched-concentration shuffled）对照和匹配预算广度扫描等预注册实验。

Result: tau²-bench上均匀稠密奖励在4/5种子中优于稀疏二值奖励；将奖励集中在进度轮或随机轮同样有害。终端验证器将信号坍缩为单个末写轮次（98% 回滚中 k=1），而实际成功需要5-8步工具调用链。合成相边界显示交叉点 V_d*~0.8，而实测 tau²-bench 为~0.15、BFCL V3 为~0.4。BFCL上混洗对照在8/8种子均为负；ToolACE-2-8B 上32个预注册种子 Delta=-0.048，独立20种子复制也显著。广度扫描显示均匀奖励的优势仅在全链覆盖时消失。

Conclusion: 在低 V_d（终端验证）场景下，统一均匀再分配奖励是零信息覆盖的默认方案，任何按轮次定位的方法都必须击败它。作者提出了匹配浓度混洗对照作为定位式信用分配声明必须通过的检验标准。

Abstract: Multi-turn agentic RL increasingly treats credit assignment as a targeting problem: given a terminal verifiable reward, per-turn methods localize credit onto the turns that mattered. We identify the structural quantity that predicts when this is the right move, the verifier information density V_d = k/C (the fraction of an agent's C-step causal chain whose per-turn correctness the verifier exposes), and show that terminal-state verifiers sit deep in a low-V_d regime where targeting is the wrong axis. In controlled shared-rollout comparisons on tau^2-bench that separate reward density from credit geometry, a continuous dense reward spread uniformly beats the sparse binary outcome reward (net-harmful on 4/5 seeds), while concentrating the same advantage on progress turns or on random turns is equally harmful: targeting is second-order. The mechanism is coverage: terminal-state verification collapses the observable signal to a single final-write turn (k=1 in 98% of rollouts) while success requires a 5-8 step chain of prerequisite tool calls. A synthetic phase boundary places the crossover at V_d* ~ 0.8, whereas measured V_d is ~0.15 on tau^2-bench and ~0.4 on BFCL V3; uniform also wins on BFCL, where a matched-concentration shuffled control is negative on 8/8 seeds. The effect reproduces across model families on ToolACE-2-8B (Delta = -0.048 over 32 pre-registered seeds; an independent 20-seed replication is itself significant), and a pre-registered matched-budget breadth sweep traces a monotone dose-response whose deficit vanishes only at full chain coverage, with a reward-to-go arm reaching full-coverage parity. Uniform redistribution is the zero-information coverage default that per-turn schemes must beat; we contribute the matched-concentration shuffled control that any targeting claim should clear.

</details>


### [58] [IFW-BLS: Dual-Robust Broad Learning System with Intuitionistic Fuzzy Wave Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02422)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IFW-BLS，一种直觉模糊小波宽度学习系统，通过有界非对称小波损失和直觉模糊评分分别解决残差主导目标和样本可靠性问题，配合Nesterov加速梯度优化，在UCI数据集上验证了优于基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BLS的最小二乘训练存在两个脆弱点：一是噪声、异常值或标签污染导致的大残差会主导目标函数；二是所有样本被平等对待，即使某些样本位于模糊或局部冲突区域。这两个问题都需要在统一优化框架内解决。

Method: 提出IFW-BLS模型，包含两个鲁棒性机制：(1) 残差级保护——用有界、平滑、非对称的小波损失替换平方损失，有界性防止极端残差影响过大，非对称性允许对正负偏差差异化惩罚；(2) 样本级可信度控制——通过直觉模糊评分结合全局类中心一致性和局部邻域冲突，对不可靠样本进行降权。使用Nesterov加速梯度优化器求解，避免传统BLS的显式矩阵求逆。

Result: 在UCI基准数据集上的实验验证了IFW-BLS优于基线模型；额外的污染实验表明其在噪声和异常值污染下比BLS具有更稳定的性能。

Conclusion: IFW-BLS通过双层次鲁棒性设计（残差级有界损失+样本级模糊可信度）有效解决了BLS在噪声和异常场景下的脆弱性问题，且避免了矩阵求逆的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Broad Learning System is an efficient randomized learning model that expands network width through feature and enhancement nodes and estimates the output weights without deep backpropagation. Its standard least-squares training, however, is vulnerable in two different ways: (i) large residuals caused by noise, outliers, or corrupted labels can dominate the objective, and (ii) all samples are treated as equally reliable even when some lie in ambiguous or locally conflicting regions. This paper proposes IFW-BLS, an Intuitionistic Fuzzy Wave Broad Learning System that addresses these two sources of fragility within one optimization model. The first robustness mechanism is residual-level protection, obtained by replacing the squared loss with the bounded, smooth, and asymmetric wave loss. Boundedness prevents extreme residuals from receiving unbounded influence, while asymmetry allows positive and negative deviations to be penalized differently when the dominant error direction varies. The second mechanism is sample-level credibility control, obtained through intuitionistic fuzzy scores that combine global class-center consistency with local neighborhood conflict. The resulting model evaluates the wave loss on credibility-weighted residuals, so unreliable samples are down-weighted before the bounded loss further limits the effect of extreme errors. A Nesterov accelerated gradient based optimizer is used to solve the proposed objective, avoiding the explicit matrix inversion used in conventional BLS. Experiments on UCI benchmark datasets validate the superiority of the proposed IFW-BLS model over the baseline models; additional corruption experiments also show more stable performance than BLS under noise and outlier contamination.

</details>


### [59] [Towards One-for-All Robustness Across a Continuum of Threat Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02440)
*Zhichao Hou,Xiaorui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出威胁条件网络（TCN），通过表示分解框架将表征学习拆分为威胁不变共享骨干和轻量级威胁条件适配器，使单个模型能够在一系列威胁级别上实现强鲁棒性，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上以仅4.6%的参数开销匹配或超越完整集成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒模型通常过度拟合特定攻击预算，导致需要多个专门模型来应对多样化和动态的对抗环境，这在威胁空间增长时变得不可行。作者旨在解决一个开放挑战：能否在单个模型中实现对连续威胁级别的强鲁棒性。

Method: TCN基于表示分解框架，将表征学习分解为威胁不变共享骨干和轻量级威胁条件适配器。通过傅里叶嵌入和通道级仿射调制对单一模型进行扰动级别条件化，并针对扰动预算分布进行训练，使模型在推理时能灵活无缝地适应无限连续的威胁级别。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上的广泛实验表明，TCN以单一参数集匹配或超越完整预算专门模型集成，能泛化到未见过的扰动预算，并在威胁条件不匹配时稳健转移，仅增加4.6%的参数开销。

Conclusion: TCN为动态和多样化威胁环境中实现自适应和可泛化的鲁棒性开辟了有前景的路径，解决了以往需要多个专门模型不可行的问题。

Abstract: Adversarially robust models often overfit to a specific attack budget, necessitating multiple specialized models for diverse and dynamic adversarial environments, a strategy that becomes fundamentally intractable as the threat space grows. This raises an open challenge: can we achieve strong robustness across a continuum of threat levels within a single model? We propose the Threat Conditional Network (TCN), grounded in a representation factorization framework that decomposes representation learning into a threat-invariant shared backbone and a lightweight threat-conditional adaptor. TCN conditions a single model on the perturbation level via Fourier-based embeddings and channel-wise affine modulation, and is trained against a distribution over perturbation budgets, enabling flexible and seamless adaptation across an infinite continuum of threat levels during inference. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet show that TCN matches or surpasses a full ensemble of budget-specialized models with a single set of parameters, generalizes to unseen perturbation budgets, and transfers robustly under mismatched threat conditions, with only 4.6\% parameter overhead. These contributions chart a promising path toward adaptive and generalizable robustness in dynamic and diverse threat environments.

</details>


### [60] [CACTUS: Mask-Guided Semantic Clean-Label Backdoors in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02450)
*Chao Feng,Burkhard Stiller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CACTUS是一种针对去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的隐蔽后门攻击方法，通过将标签一致的语义对转换为目标导向的表示偏移，利用掩码引导的模态特定算子将触发效果跨样本耦合，并在peer聚合前对干净的非目标嵌入进行反事实偏移应用。在语音、文本、表格和图像四种模态及九种聚合规则下验证，30%恶意节点时在语音任务上达到51.2%的平均攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的语义触发攻击比合成补丁更隐蔽，但样本依赖的放置方式可能削弱跨聚合轮次的后门植入效果；在去中心化联邦学习中，拓扑依赖的peer聚合会反复混合本地模型，使这一问题更加严峻。

Method: CACTUS将标签一致的语义对转化为目标导向的表示偏移；通过掩码引导、模态特定的算子来隔离触发效应，将触发效应跨样本耦合，并在peer聚合前以反事实方式将偏移应用于干净的非目标嵌入。

Result: 实验覆盖语音、文本、表格和图像四种任务及九种聚合规则。在30%恶意节点下，CACTUS在Speech Commands上达到九种规则平均51.2%的攻击成功率，并在四种模态中的三种上达到最高平均ASR。敏感性分析表明ASR随网络拓扑变化并随恶意节点比例增加而上升。

Conclusion: CACTUS能够通过反复的DFL聚合传播后门，表明其在去中心化联邦学习场景中具有强韧的攻击性能。

Abstract: Semantic triggers in federated learning (FL) can be less conspicuous than synthetic patches, but sample-dependent placement may weaken backdoor implantation across aggregation rounds. This challenge is compounded in decentralized FL (DFL), where topology-dependent peer aggregation repeatedly mixes local models. CACTUS converts label-consistent semantic pairs into target-directed representation shifts. Mask-guided, modality-specific operators isolate trigger effects, couple them across samples, and apply the shifts counterfactually to clean non-target embeddings before peer aggregation. Experiments cover speech, text, tabular, and image tasks under nine aggregation rules. With 30\% malicious nodes, CACTUS reaches a nine-rule mean attack success rate (ASR) of 51.2\% on Speech Commands and the highest nine-rule mean ASR among evaluated attacks on three of four modalities. Sensitivity analyses show that ASR varies with network topology and increases with the malicious-node ratio. These results indicate that CACTUS can propagate backdoors through repeated DFL aggregation.

</details>


### [61] [Scalable Kronecker-Fisher Approximation: Efficient Hessian Analysis for Billion-Parameter Language Models Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02451)
*Viacheslav Yusupov,Daria Cherniuk,Evgeny Frolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Kronecker分解的可扩展近似方法，在不存储完整Fisher信息矩阵的情况下捕捉跨层交互，从而实现对十亿参数规模网络的海森矩阵分析，并揭示不同模型家族中value投影层的高敏感性和强跨层相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿参数规模的神经网络中，完整Fisher信息矩阵的计算和存储不可行，导致难以进行实际的海森分析来识别模型脆弱组件，需要一种可扩展的近似方案。

Method: 采用Kronecker分解的近似方法，避免存储完整Fisher信息矩阵，同时能够捕捉跨层权重之间的交互作用。

Result: 发现了一致的脆弱性模式：value投影层具有最高的敏感性和最强的跨层相关性（跨多个模型家族一致），而其他组件呈现架构特化的行为。通过量化、稀疏化、层间破坏和破坏后微调等实验验证了该近似与性能退化及恢复强相关。

Conclusion: 该框架为识别大型模型中的脆弱组件提供实用且有理论依据的工具，可指导混合精度分配、逐层稀疏化和自适应低秩分解等压缩与优化策略。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a scalable Kronecker-based approximation that captures cross-layer interactions without storing the entire Fisher matrix, enabling practical Hessian analysis for billion-parameter networks where full computation is infeasible. Our approach reveals consistent vulnerability patterns: value projection layers exhibit the highest sensitivity and strongest cross-layer correlations across multiple model families, while other components exhibit architecture-specific behaviors. Through extensive experiments on quantization, sparsification, inter-layer corruption, and post-corruption fine-tuning, we demonstrate that our approximation strongly correlates with both performance degradation and recovery. Our framework provides a practical, theoretically grounded tool for identifying fragile components in large models, opening new avenues for guided compression and optimization strategies, such as mixed-precision allocation, layer-wise sparsity, and adaptive low-rank decomposition across layers and even individual weight groups.

</details>


### [62] [DeepAffinity: Long-Term Aspect Preference Prediction in eCommerce using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02468)
*Yotam Eshel,Guy Hadad,Guy Feigenblat,Yuri M. Brovman,Matt Gearhart,Bracha Shapira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DeepAffinity的方法，用于预测电商用户对产品属性（如品牌、尺寸、颜色）的偏好。该方法利用小型语言模型（SLMs）配合结构化提示和专用预测头进行微调，在时间序列预测任务上优于标准生成式微调方法，并能提升大规模跨国电商平台的推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商个性化主要关注产品层面的推荐，缺乏对用户产品属性（如品牌、尺寸、颜色）偏好的细粒度理解和预测能力。提升这一能力有助于更好地理解用户，并在推荐、搜索和营销中实现精细化个性化，因此需要专门的任务定义和解决方案。

Method: 将"Aspect Affinity"定义为时间预测任务：基于用户按时间排序的交互历史预测其未来的属性选择。提出DeepAffinity框架，使用小型语言模型（SLMs），结合结构化提示和专门的预测头进行任务微调，而非传统的生成式微调。

Result: DeepAffinity在性能上优于标准生成式微调方法；通用开源大型语言模型（LLMs）在无任务特定微调的情况下表现不佳，难以建模细微的用户行为；此外DeepAffinity在大规模跨国电商平台上有效提升了推荐质量。

Conclusion: DeepAffinity通过SLMs和任务定制的方法有效解决了Aspect Affinity预测问题，证明了在细粒度用户偏好建模任务中，小型定向模型优于通用大模型的实用性，并具有显著的工业应用价值。

Abstract: We explore predicting eCommerce user preferences for product aspects such as brand, size, and color - a task we define as Aspect Affinity. Solving this task improves customer understanding and enables fine-grained personalization in recommendation, search, and marketing. We frame Aspect Affinity as a temporal prediction task: forecasting a users future aspect choices from their time-ordered interaction history, capturing long-term preferences that evolve beyond the current session. To this end, we propose DeepAffinity, which leverages Small Language Models (SLMs) with structured prompts and specialized prediction heads fine-tuned for this task. We show DeepAffinity outperforms standard generative fine-tuning methods, while general-purpose open-source LLMs perform poorly without task-specific tuning, highlighting their limits in modeling nuanced behavior. Finally, DeepAffinity enhances recommendation quality on a large-scale multinational eCommerce platform.

</details>


### [63] [RINSE: Robust Target-Time Normality Estimation for Zero-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02497)
*Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Md Abrar Jahin,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RINSE（鲁棒迭代正态性自估计）框架，用于零样本图异常检测。该方法在保持源训练检测器不变的前提下，通过梯度无关的目标时处理，迭代估计目标图的正态性、表征校准和证据可靠性，最终在8个未见目标图上取得了最高的平均AUPRC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本图异常检测需要将在源图上训练的检测器部署到未见且无标签的目标图，但域偏移可能导致源自源域的"正态性"定义在目标域上失效，影响检测性能。

Method: RINSE通过三个核心步骤实现：1）从目标图中识别低残差的可信赖节点子集，构建裁剪后的目标感知正态性模型；2）通过可靠性门控的秩融合和编码器集成来整合互补的异常证据；3）整个流程无需梯度计算和每个目标单独调参。

Result: 在8个未见目标图上，RINSE在两种独立预处理协议下均取得所有评估方法中最高的平均AUPRC，且消融实验和敏感性分析验证了各设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持"无需目标标签、梯度或逐目标调参的鲁棒目标时估计"是实现通用图异常检测的实用途径。

Abstract: Zero-shot graph anomaly detection seeks to deploy a detector trained on source graphs to unseen, unlabeled targets, yet domain shift can make source-derived notions of normality unreliable. We introduce RINSE (Robust Iterative Normality Self-Estimation), a gradient-free target-time framework that keeps the source-trained detector fixed while sequentially estimating target normality, representation calibration, and evidence reliability from the target graph. Its core idea is to identify a reliable subset of low-residual target nodes, use them to construct a trimmed target-aware normality model, and combine complementary anomaly evidence through reliability-gated rank fusion and encoder ensembling. Across eight unseen target graphs, RINSE achieves the highest average AUPRC among the evaluated methods under two separate preprocessing protocols, while block ablations and sensitivity analyses support the combined design. These results support robust target-time estimation as a practical approach to generalist graph anomaly detection without target labels, gradients, or per-target tuning.

</details>


### [64] [Rethinking the Teacher-Student Framework for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02507)
*Damian Sójka,Marc Masana,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了测试时自适应（TTA）中教师-学生框架默认使用指数移动平均更新教师权重的做法，提出使用不更新权重的"固执教师"（intransigent teacher），证明这一简单改动能显著提升长序列场景下的性能并增强对超参数的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法虽广泛采用教师-学生框架来缓解误差累积，但其长期稳定性常被忽视。作者发现即使在教师-学生框架下，误差累积仍然发生在比常用场景更长的序列中，因此需要重新审视教师权重的更新策略。

Method: 作者分析教师-学生框架中的稳定性-可塑性权衡，提出使用完全不更新权重的"固执教师"（intransigent teacher），取代默认的指数移动平均（EMA）教师策略，并验证该改动可无缝应用于多种架构和实验设置。

Result: 该简单改动在多个数据集的长序列场景下显著提升了TTA方法性能，同时增强了对超参数变化的鲁棒性，并成功应用于不同架构和语义分割等实验设置中。

Conclusion: 固定不变的教师权重比EMA更新的教师更能有效抑制误差累积，为TTA中教师-学生框架的设计提供了新的洞见和简单有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) has recently emerged as a promising strategy that allows the adaptation of pre-trained models to changing data distributions at deployment time, without access to any labels. To mitigate error accumulation, researchers have widely adopted the teacher-student framework, though its long-term stability is often taken for granted. In this work, we challenge the common strategy of setting the teacher weights to an exponential moving average of the student by showing that error accumulation still occurs, although it is mostly apparent on longer sequences compared to those commonly utilized. We analyze the stability-plasticity trade-off within the teacher-student framework and propose to use an intransigent teacher that does not update its weights. Surprisingly, we show that this simple change allows TTA methods to significantly improve their performance on multiple datasets with longer scenarios and result in increased robustness to changes in hyperparameters. Finally, we show that those changes can be seamlessly and effectively applied to various architectures and experimental setups, including semantic segmentation. The code is available at https://github.com/dmn-sjk/intransigent_teacher.

</details>


### [65] [Spectral Initialization and Scheduled Graph Smoothness for Uncertain Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02519)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Jay Pujara,Craig A. Knoblock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUEST通过引入置信度加权图拉普拉斯的谱先验（最小的非平凡特征向量）初始化实体嵌入，并结合无偏小批量Dirichlet能量正则化器，在不增加可训练参数的情况下提升了不确定知识图谱补全的准确性和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定知识图谱（UKGs）为每条三元组分配连续置信度分数，但大多数可能的三元组缺乏观测置信度，现有半监督方法生成伪标签时忽略了置信度加权图所蕴含的全局社区结构和枢纽结构。

Method: QUEST在标准置信度分布学习流程上不加任何可训练参数：1) 利用置信度加权图拉普拉斯的最小非平凡特征向量初始化实体嵌入，以纳入社区和枢纽结构；2) 应用无偏的小批量Dirichlet能量正则化器，强制训练早期阶段的结构一致性。

Result: 在两个UKG数据集上，QUEST在八个度量-数据集组合中的六个上超越了先前方法的置信度预测和链接预测性能，在剩余两个组合上持平最优结果，同时消除了稠密图上观测到的训练不稳定尖峰。

Conclusion: 谱结构先验结合图Dirichlet能量正则化器能够提升UKG补全的准确性、训练稳定性及检查点可靠性。

Abstract: Uncertain knowledge graphs (UKGs) extend knowledge graphs by assigning each triple a continuous confidence score. Since most possible triples lack observed confidences, recent methods rely on semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels. These methods initialize entity embeddings without using the confidence-weighted graph, discarding its global community and hub structure. We introduce QUEST, which adds no trainable parameters to the standard confidence-distribution learning pipeline. First, QUEST initializes entity embeddings using the smallest non-trivial eigenvectors of the confidence-weighted graph Laplacian, incorporating community and hub structure before training. Second, QUEST applies an unbiased mini-batch Dirichlet energy regularizer to enforce early-stage structural consistency. On two UKG datasets, QUEST improves confidence prediction and link prediction on six of eight metric-dataset pairs over prior methods and matches the previous best on the remaining two, while removing the instability spike observed on dense graphs. These results indicate that spectral structural priors combined with a graph Dirichlet energy regularizer improve accuracy, training stability, and checkpoint reliability in UKG completion.

</details>


### [66] [A Comparative Study of Graph Representations for GNN-Based Power Grid Control in L2RPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02538)
*Adrian Degenkolb,Qiong Huang,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度强化学习中电网控制任务的图构建问题，通过受控实验比较了物理拓扑、电气灵敏度及混合变体等多种图表示方法在L2RPN环境中的表现。核心发现是：图复杂度与任务粒度的匹配比最大化表示丰富度更重要，并强调了在大规模场景下进行受控表示研究的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图构建是深度强化学习用于电网控制任务中一个关键但未被充分研究的设计选择，现有工作中缺乏对不同图表示方法的系统性、受控比较。

Method: 在L2RPN（Learning to Run a Power Network）环境中，对多种图表示方法进行受控实验对比，包括物理拓扑图、电气灵敏度图以及两者的混合变体，用于拓扑控制任务。

Result: 结果表明，图复杂度与任务粒度的匹配程度比最大化图的表示丰富度更为关键；同时证明了在大规模场景下开展受控表示研究的价值。

Conclusion: 在电网控制的深度强化学习任务中，选择图表示时应优先考虑其与任务粒度的匹配性，而非单纯追求表示的信息丰富度，这为后续此类系统研究提供了方向指引。

Abstract: Graph construction is a critical but underexamined design choice in deep reinforcement learning for power grid control. We present a controlled experimental comparison of different graph representations, including physical topology, electrical-sensitivity, and hybrid variants for topology control in the Learning to Run a Power Network (L2RPN) environment. Our findings indicate that matching graph complexity to task granularity is more important than maximizing representational richness, and highlight the importance of controlled representation studies at scale.

</details>


### [67] [TrajMind: Chaining Role-Specialized LoRAs for Fast-and-Slow Collective Trajectory Anomaly Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02540)
*Jiahao Wu,Zhenqun Yang,Chen Jason Zhang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrajMind是一个"快-慢"双路径框架，用于从城市轨迹中诊断集体异常。慢路径TrajMind_slow通过画布式分类、条件定位和可执行验证提供结构化的可验证诊断，快路径TrajMind_fast在单个文本通道中筛查每个窗口以提供低延迟警报。实验表明慢路径在异常分类和定位上分别超越最强基线15.3和13.8个百分点，快路径降低41.1%延迟且保持93.5%以上的二元平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测器只能产生分数或标签，而视觉-语言管道虽能提供丰富语义，但无法将可验证诊断与低延迟监控结合。核心挑战在于无需对每个监控窗口运行完整诊断管道，就能从源轨迹中识别集体模式并恢复精确事件细节。

Method: 将始终在线的筛查与按需诊断分离：筛查负责发出警报，诊断仅发布经源验证的"什么-谁-何处-何时"记录。TrajMind采用"快-慢"框架，在一个冻结的视觉-语言骨干上切换三个角色专用的LoRA适配器。慢路径链式执行画布分类、序列化轨迹上的条件定位和可执行验证；快路径以单一纯文本通道筛查每一窗口。

Result: TrajMind_slow在异常分类上比最强基线高至少15.3个百分点，定位上高13.8个百分点；这些提升在跨城市迁移中保持。TrajMind_fast将延迟降低41.1%，并维持至少93.5%的二元平衡准确率。

Conclusion: TrajMind在跨城市场景下提供准确、有证据支撑的诊断，以及高效的前线监控能力，实现了可验证诊断与低延迟监测的有效统一。

Abstract: Diagnosing collective anomalies from urban trajectories is increasingly important for traffic governance, as it reveals what happened, who was involved, and where and when the event occurred. Existing detectors efficiently produce scores or labels, whereas vision--language pipelines provide richer semantics; neither couples verifiable diagnosis with low-latency monitoring. The central challenge is to recognize collective patterns and recover exact event details from the source trajectories without running the full diagnostic pipeline for every monitored window. We therefore separate always-on screening from on-demand diagnosis: screening raises alerts, while diagnosis releases only source-verified what--who--where--when records. We present TrajMind, a fast-and-slow framework that switches three role-specialized LoRA adapters over one frozen vision--language backbone. Its slow path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{slow}}$}, chains canvas-based typing, type-conditioned localization over serialized trajectories, and executable verification, yielding structured, evidence-backed diagnoses. Additionally, the fast path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{fast}}$}, screens each window in a single text-only pass, delivering efficient structured alerts. Extensive experiments show that, TrajMind$_{\mathrm{slow}}$ outperforms the strongest baselines by at least $15.3$ percentage points in anomaly typing and $13.8$ percentage points in localization. These gains persist under cross-city transfer, and TrajMind$_{\mathrm{fast}}$ reduces latency by $41.1\%$ and maintains binary balanced accuracy of at least $93.5\%$. Together, TrajMind delivers accurate, evidence-backed diagnoses across cities and efficient front-line monitoring.

</details>


### [68] [Learn from Whoever Is Right: Answer-Verified Multi-Teacher Distillation for Multi-Domain LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02548)
*Xixiang He,Xingming Li,Baiqi Wu,Qiyao Sun,Xuanyu Ji,Ao Cheng,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-SDPO（多教师自蒸馏策略优化），通过逐样本识别可靠教师而非按领域匹配教师，将多个冻结教师模型统一蒸馏为一个学生模型。该方法包含自锚定、答案验证的资格判定和特权蒸馏三个组件，实验表明在Qwen3-8B等五个学生模型上显著缩小领域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法按领域标签将样本路由给对应领域的教师模型，但领域专长仅是平均意义上的：匹配的教师并非在每个样本上都正确，而其他领域的教师有时反而正确。因此需要逐样本识别可靠教师，而非逐领域识别。

Method: MT-SDPO包含三个组件：(1) 自锚定——用同组中正确的rollout来监督当前rollout；(2) 答案验证的资格判定——教师只有在自己答案通过验证器时才能监督某样本；(3) 特权蒸馏——将锚定和所有验证通过的反馈合并到一个上下文中，由EMA自教师读取而学生不可读，从而在部署时保持单一策略。

Result: 在三个模型家族的五个学生模型上，MT-SDPO使Qwen3-8B最弱领域提升14.79分，领域差距缩小74.7%，优于按领域提供匹配教师的方法。

Conclusion: 应由验证过的可靠性而非领域归属来决定谁来教授。代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) rely on reinforcement learning to build strong capabilities in individual domains, but integrating those capabilities into a single deployable model remains challenging. By routing each sample to the teacher whose domain matches it, existing approaches let a domain label decide which teacher provides supervision. However, domain expertise holds only on average: the matched teacher is not always correct on a given sample, while a teacher from another domain sometimes is. The reliable teacher therefore has to be identified per sample, not per domain. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Teacher Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (MT-SDPO), an on-policy distillation method that unifies several frozen teachers into one student model. MT-SDPO consists of three components: (1) self-anchors, where a rollout is supervised by a correct rollout from its own group; (2) answer-verified eligibility, where a teacher may supervise a sample only if its own answer passes a verifier; and (3) privileged distillation, which merges the anchor and all verified feedback into one context that an exponential moving average self-teacher reads and the student does not, thereby keeping one policy at deployment. Across five students from three model families, MT-SDPO lifts the weakest domain of Qwen3-8B by 14.79 points and narrows its domain gap by 74.7%, a better balance than serving one matched teacher per domain. Verified reliability, not domain membership, should decide who teaches. Code is available at https://github.com/hexixiang/MT-SDPO.

</details>


### [69] [ProbeMatchDTI: Probe-Driven Multi-Scale Biochemical Pattern Matching for Drug-Target Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02549)
*Quan Hao,Mengyue Fan,Zifan Dong,Youru Li,Jianduo Zhao,Lechuan Xu,Hao Zhang,Fei Xia,Jigang Wang,Chong Qiu,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProbeMatchDTI框架，通过IterProbe和BindingProbe两种模式探针机制，在DTI预测中保留弱生化信号并进行跨尺度模式匹配，在BindingDB和DrugBank上分别提升AUC-ROC 2.0%和0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生化表示学习方法在DTI预测中采用被动特征聚合，倾向于突出主导分子模式而抑制弱的但结合相关的信号（如功能基团和残基上下文模式），限制了多尺度生化对应关系的建模能力。

Method: 提出ProbeMatchDTI框架，包含两个核心模块：IterProbe通过可学习探针在细化深度内显式保留上下文状态并在每个位置选择这些状态，再进行跨实体匹配，以保留弱生化模式并加强功能基团、局部基序和分子骨架之间的关联；BindingProbe在局部生化单元和整对层面刻画药物-蛋白质互补性，联合建模细粒度交互和多尺度对应关系，同时保留较弱的结合相关关联。

Result: 在BindingDB上AUC-ROC提升2.0%，在DrugBank上提升0.5%。特征级模式分析验证了探针驱动的跨尺度生化模式匹配行为，并通过证据引导的下游药物发现工作流程展示了预测结果的实用性。

Conclusion: ProbeMatchDTI通过探针驱动的模式保留和跨尺度匹配机制，有效改善了DTI预测中对弱结合相关生化信号的建模，并在实际药物发现工作流中展示了应用价值。

Abstract: Drug-target interaction (DTI) prediction is an important task in AI-driven drug discovery. Although recent biochemical representation learning methods have improved DTI prediction, their passive feature aggregation tends to favor dominant molecular patterns while suppressing weak yet binding-relevant signals, such as functional groups and residue-context patterns, limiting the modeling of multi-scale biochemical correspondences. To address this issue, we propose ProbeMatchDTI, a pattern-probe-driven framework comprising IterProbe and BindingProbe. IterProbe explicitly retains contextual states across refinement depths and uses learnable probes to select them at each position before cross-entity matching, thereby preserving weak biochemical patterns and strengthening associations among functional groups, local motifs, and molecular scaffolds. BindingProbe then characterizes cross-entity drug-protein complementarity at local biochemical-unit and whole-pair levels, jointly modeling fine-grained interactions and multi-scale correspondences while preserving weaker binding-relevant associations. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of ProbeMatchDTI, achieving 2.0% and 0.5% higher AUC-ROC on BindingDB and DrugBank, respectively. Feature-level pattern analyses further characterize its probe-driven behavior in cross-scale biochemical pattern matching. We further connect ProbeMatchDTI predictions with an evidence-guided downstream drug-discovery workflow, demonstrating their utility for candidate refinement and validation planning. Our code is available at https://github.com/developer-hq/ProbeMatchDTI

</details>


### [70] [Online Reinforcement Learning in the Met Office Unified Model through Distributed Model-Agent Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02566)
*Pritthijit Nath,Sebastian Schemm,Peter Haynes,Emily Shuckburgh,Mark Webb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将机器学习修正与全球数值天气预报模型耦合的方法：通过分布式强化学习（DDPG）智能体对Met Office统一模型（UM）的倾向性施加有界位温校正，采用秩局部张量跨70个垂直层共享权重。在十次带松弛训练的预报中，以UKMO业务分析为反事实目标，训练完成后在无松弛的预报中评估冻结策略。结果表明该方法顺利完成训练且数值稳定，+6小时预报中Z500 MAE在六个纬度带中的四个得到降低（南北热带分别降低45.8%和40.8%），MSLP误差在三个纬度带减少（0-30°N最大降幅27.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数值天气预报模型存在系统性偏差，而机器学习校正要实用化必须适应演化中的模型状态，同时保持动力学一致性和数值稳定性。作者希望通过分布式在线学习与业务模型的紧耦合来验证这一可行性。

Method: 将Met Office统一模型（UM）与分布式RL智能体通过秩局部张量耦合。DDPG actor在每列大气70个垂直层间共享权重，对模型倾向施加有界的位温校正。训练阶段：在十个带松弛的预报中，将计算向UKMO业务分析进行nudging以提供反事实目标。推理阶段：冻结策略在无松弛的预报中进行评估。

Result: 耦合工作流成功完成训练并在评估案例中保持数值稳定。相对匹配的原生UM预报，+6小时时学习策略在六个纬度带中的四个降低Z500 MAE，南北热带分别减少45.8%和40.8%。MSLP误差在三个纬度带下降，0-30°N最大降幅27.3%。

Conclusion: 该单案例实验展示了分布式在线学习后进行无松弛推理的显著潜力和可行性，为基于强化学习的偏差校正和参数化方案在业务系统中落地奠定了基础。

Abstract: Machine-learnt corrections can complement numerical weather prediction only if they adapt to the evolving model state while preserving dynamical consistency and numerical stability. To test this within a global forecasting model, we couple the Met Office (UKMO) Unified Model (UM) with distributed RL agents through rank-local tensors. A DDPG actor shares weights across the 70 vertical model levels of each atmospheric column and applies bounded potential-temperature corrections to the model tendencies. Across ten nudged training forecasts, nudging calculations towards the UKMO operational analysis provides an immediate counterfactual target. The frozen policy is then evaluated in a non-nudged forecast for inference. The coupled workflow successfully completes training and remains numerically stable in the evaluated case. Relative to a matched native UM forecast at +6 h, the learnt policy reduces Z$_{500}$ MAE in four of six latitude bands, including reductions of 45.8% and 40.8% in the northern and southern tropics. MSLP error too decreases in three bands, with a maximum reduction of 27.3% at 0-30°N. This single-case experiment demonstrates significant promise and feasibility of distributed online learning followed by non-nudged inference, laying the groundwork for RL-based bias correction and parametrisations within operational systems.

</details>


### [71] [Source Distribution Estimation by Posterior Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02622)
*Trung-Dung Hoang,Lisa M. Koch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于期望最大化（EM）的源分布估计（SDE）方法，通过交替执行E步（基于当前源估计生成新仿真训练摊销后验）和M步（用后验均值重拟合源分布），解决了现有方法依赖固定提议先验训练似然代理而导致的参数空间失准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真科学中常需估计模拟器参数分布，使参数经模拟器前向传播后能再现真实观测。现有方法使用固定提议先验训练似然代理，目标函数仅基于代理而非真实模拟器，在代理未训练的参数区域表现不佳。作者希望解决这一代理失准问题。

Method: 采用期望最大化（EM）框架求解源分布估计问题：E步利用当前源估计生成新的仿真数据，训练一个摊销后验；M步基于观测数据的后验平均重新拟合源分布。提供两种参数化方式：(1) 独立的源流和后验流；(2) 共享的单一条件流。

Result: 在三个基准任务上，分别使用宽先验和错误设定的初始先验进行评估。两种方法均优于现有固定代理方法及其迭代变体。在Lotka-Volterra任务中优势最为显著：所有基线方法数据空间C2ST均不低于0.96，而本文方法在四种初始先验设置中的三种达到0.64-0.68。

Conclusion: 基于EM框架的源分布估计方法通过动态更新仿真分布，有效克服了固定代理方法在失准参数区域的局限，显著提升了仿真参数分布估计的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Simulation-based science often requires a distribution over simulator parameters whose push-forward reproduces a set of real observations: this is the source distribution estimation (SDE) problem. Existing methods fit the source against a likelihood surrogate trained once from a fixed proposal prior. Their objective is therefore stated only in terms of the surrogate instead of the true simulator, which may fail for inaccurate areas in parameter space where the surrogate was never trained. We instead solve SDE by expectation maximization: an E-step trains an amortized posterior on fresh simulations from the current source estimate, and an M-step refits the source to the average of that posterior over the observed data. We give two parameterizations, (1) separate source and posterior flows and (2) a single shared conditional flow. We evaluate our method on three benchmark tasks under both broad and misspecified initial priors. Both improve on existing fixed surrogate approaches and on iterated variants of each, most clearly on Lotka--Volterra, where no baseline falls below 0.96 data-space C2ST while our methods reach 0.64-0.68 in three of four initial-prior settings.

</details>


### [72] [Oracle, will I ever learn? A study of prediction convergence and complementarity across link prediction models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02638)
*Guillaume Méroué,Fabien Gandon,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"神谕"（oracle）方法衡量知识图谱链接预测模型间的互补性，发现不同模型确实能捕获互补知识，但这种互补性在增加模型数量时会迅速饱和，始终存在无法被集体解决的查询子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链接预测模型在相同查询上会产生不同预测结果，暗示不同模型可能捕获了互补的知识，但尚不清楚这种互补性有多大、能否通过模型组合来恢复这些知识。这引发了一个基本问题：不同模型在多大程度上捕获互补知识，以及其中多少可以通过组合模型来恢复。

Method: 提出通过"神谕"（oracle）来衡量模型互补性：对于每个查询，神谕从一组模型中选出最佳预测，从而提供模型组合可达到的性能上界。跨多种架构和基准数据集进行比较分析。

Result: 发现个体模型与神谕之间存在显著差距，表明不同模型确实捕获了互补知识；但这种互补性随着模型数量增加会迅速饱和，留下即使大量模型也无法解决的持续未解查询子集。

Conclusion: 研究既揭示了模型互补性的潜力，也揭示了当前链接预测模型集体恢复能力的根本局限，强调了构建稳健Web应用所需进一步研究的必要性。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become an important source of structured knowledge for Web applications, including search, question answering, and recommender systems. In these applications, link prediction can serve either as a prediction task itself or as a means to enrich incomplete knowledge graphs for downstream tasks. Interestingly, different link prediction models, or even different training runs of the same model, can produce substantially different predictions for the same query. This suggests a variability in the capture of the underlying knowledge by models, thus raising a fundamental question: to what extent do different models capture complementary knowledge, and how much of this knowledge could be recovered by combining them? We propose to measure model complementarity through the performance of an oracle that, for each query, selects the best prediction among a considered set of models, hence providing an upper bound on the performance achievable through model combination. Across several architectures and benchmarks, we find a substantial gap between individual models and their oracle, revealing that different models capture complementary knowledge. Yet, this complementarity rapidly saturates as more models are added, leaving a persistent subset of queries unsolved even by a large number of models. These findings reveal both the potential of model complementarity and a fundamental limit to what current link prediction models can collectively recover; thereby highlighting the need for further research to build robust Web applications.

</details>


### [73] [Differentiable Electricity-Market Clearing for Gradient-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02646)
*Luca Mungo,Maarten P. Scholl,Arnau Quera-Bofarull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电力市场出清建模为可微优化层，使规划者能够通过反向传播梯度来优化大型数据中心选址规划，并以50MW负载在6个候选节点的分配问题验证了该方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心规划困难，因为其规模会反过来影响所支付的实时电价（由市场出清决定）。现有模拟方法只能评估候选方案的表现，却无法给出改进方向，缺乏一个能直接引导规划优化的技术手段。

Method: 将市场出清视为一个可微优化层：前向传播求解市场清仓问题，反向模式自动微分将规划成本通过清算价格传播回规划变量，从而计算梯度并指导优化。在合成电力网络上，对50MW负载在6个候选母线的分配问题进行梯度优化。

Result: 与穷举所有选址组合相比，梯度优化几乎精确恢复了连续分配方案，最坏情况目标差距仅为最佳与最差单一选址成本差的2.3%和8.5%。该方法唯一的系统性误差出现在站点应关闭的成本附近：平滑松弛导致站点逐渐缩小而非立即关闭，使离散转换延迟出现。

Conclusion: 可微市场出清将市场感知的规划问题转化为梯度可搜索的优化问题，为大型数据中心选址等市场耦合规划任务提供了新的求解范式。

Abstract: Planning a large data center is difficult because a facility big enough to matter changes the electricity prices it will pay. Those prices are set by market clearing, a constrained optimization problem solved anew in every operating condition. However, simulating the market tells a planner how a candidate plan performs but not how to improve it. Here we treat market clearing as a differentiable optimization layer: each forward pass solves the market, and reverse-mode automatic differentiation propagates the planning cost back through the cleared prices to the plan. After validating these gradients against finite differences, we apply them to a concrete problem: allocating 50 MW of data-center load across six candidate buses in two synthetic networks, under a fixed cost per active site, evaluated over 36 operating states. Judged against exhaustive enumeration of all site combinations, gradient optimization recovers the continuous allocations almost exactly, with worst-case objective gaps of 2.3\% and 8.5\% of the cost difference between the best and worst single site. Its one systematic error is instructive: near the costs at which a site should close, the smooth relaxation of the discrete site count shrinks the site rather than closing it, so discrete transitions arrive late. Differentiable market clearing thus turns market-aware planning into a problem gradients can search.

</details>


### [74] [Unfolding the Leech Lattice: Fused Multi-Shell Decoding and VRAM Layouts for 2-Bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02652)
*Pier-Jean Malandrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Leech格向量量化（2-bit质量最强的编码方案）缺少多壳解码器的问题，实现了完整的301类码本服务路径。作者将"VRAM内比特率"与"磁盘比特率"区分开，在恒定带宽下测试了四种比特级精确布局，发现二进制位平面在体积和速度上均优于one-hot掩码（4.80 bits/weight，为FP16的2.15倍）。与部署的4-bit（AWQ）和2-bit（QTIP）GEMV内核对比，trellis内核读取字节量少2.40倍，速度快2.27倍，时间差距与流量差距成正比。在输出头一致条件下，端到端在4B、8B、14B规模分别获得1.11x、1.29x、1.41x加速；使用int8输出头时4B模型达87.0 tok/s（占用2.60 GB）。质量代价（4B时1.38x困惑度和14.7 MMLU点损失）随模型规模增大而缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech格向量量化宣称拥有最强的2-bit质量，但其现有内核仅能解码单个壳层，而实际码率需要多壳解码器，且目前缺乏现成实现与测量。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供多壳解码器的实现并评估其在batch 1下decode-phase GEMV的服务成本。

Method: 1) 构建完整301类码本的GPU serving路径：离线展开为GPU布局，并设计无warp divergenge的融合dequantize-plus-matvec内核，以f64精度验证。2) 区分VRAM内比特率与磁盘比特率两个设计维度，在同一进程中对比四种bit-exact布局，识别二进制位平面优于one-hot掩码，并在低于4.3 bits时引入不规则流，3.6 bits以下解码不再仅靠移位与掩码。3) 与AWQ(4-bit)和QTIP(2-bit)内核同进程对比，分析字节流量与时延差距的关联。4) 通过无权重对照组和双内存层次验证有效性边界。

Result: 多壳解码服务路径在batch 1下实现4.80 bits/weight（FP16的2.15倍），二进制位平面在大小与速度上均优于one-hot掩码。trellis内核比served布局少读2.40倍字节并快2.27倍，时间差距与流量差距一致。在输出头一致时端到端提升1.11x（4B）、1.29x（8B）、1.41x（14B）；int8输出头下4B达87.0 tok/s（2.60 GB）。质量代价随规模缩小。

Conclusion: Leech格多壳解码器具有实际可行的serving价值，其优势随模型规模增大而增强。然而其内核速度上限受限于launch geometry（无权重对照组也如此），且在不同内存层次上可能低于FP16。码本过大导致无法使用查找表展开是速度瓶颈的根源，质量代价在更大模型上可被有效摊薄。

Abstract: Leech-lattice vector quantization holds the strongest reported 2-bit quality under its own evaluation protocol. Its kernel decodes one shell; we found no implementation of the multi-shell decoder the rate requires. This paper supplies one and measures its serving cost for decode-phase GEMV at batch 1. First, a serving path for the full 301-class codebook: an offline expansion into GPU layouts and a fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel reading them without warp divergence, verified against f64. Second, the in-VRAM rate is a design axis distinct from the on-disk rate. Four bit-exact layouts timed in one process show binary bit planes beating one-hot masks on size and speed at constant bandwidth (4.80 bits per weight, 2.15x FP16). Below 4.3 bits a second, irregular stream enters; at 3.6 the decode stops being shifts and masks. Third, deployed four-bit (AWQ) and two-bit (QTIP) GEMV kernels run in the same process. The trellis kernel reads 2.40x fewer bytes than our served layout and runs 2.27x faster at near-equal fractions of their byte bounds: the time gap tracks the traffic gap, the price of unfolding a codebook too large for a lookup table. Fourth, the validity envelope: the trellis kernel outruns our no-weights control, so our launch geometry sets that floor, and on a second memory hierarchy every lattice arm falls below FP16. With the output head held identical across arms, the kernel-and-format path gains 1.11x, 1.29x and 1.41x end to end at 4B, 8B and 14B; with an int8 output head the served 4B reaches 87.0 tok/s in 2.60 GB. The quality cost, 1.38x perplexity and 14.7 MMLU points at 4B, shrinks across the three sizes measured.

</details>


### [75] [H3DNAS: Hardware-Aware ONNX-Native 3D Point Cloud Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02684)
*Anchit Mulye,Rhythm Baghel,Sujay Kumar Ingle,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H3DNAS是一个无需原始源代码的硬件感知模型压缩框架，直接在ONNX计算图上对3D点云模型进行通道剪枝和结构突变，在ModelNet40上将PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，推理速度提升1.29-1.99倍且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano等边缘硬件上部署3D点云模型受到计算和内存预算的严重制约，而现有压缩方法需要访问模型的原始源代码，无法适用于厂商和模型仓库通常分发的ONNX二进制文件。

Method: 提出三大核心贡献：(1) Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)，将ONNX算子分为四类约束类型，形式化证明自由参数比例是拓扑不变量，可在O(|V|+|E|)时间内计算出可压缩上限；(2) 两阶段层次化搜索，先用L1重要性通道选择剪枝候选架构，再用零样本标签无关的输出保真度排序，最后对Pareto最优候选应用GhostConv结构突变；(3) 首个无需源代码的3D点云模型压缩流水线，完全通过ONNX图手术实现。

Result: 在ModelNet40数据集上，PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，推理速度分别提升1.99倍、1.29倍和1.67倍，且精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: H3DNAS证明了无需源代码即可在ONNX计算图上有效压缩3D点云模型，为边缘硬件部署提供了一种实用的模型压缩方案。

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud models on edge hardware such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano is severely constrained by compute and memory budgets. Existing compression methods require access to the model's original source code, rendering them inapplicable to the Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) binaries commonly distributed by vendors and model repositories. We present \textbf{H3DNAS}, a hardware-aware model compression framework that operates directly on ONNX computational graphs without requiring original source code, architecture class definition, or gradient access during search. H3DNAS makes three contributions: (1) a \textbf{Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)} that classifies ONNX operators into four constraint classes and formally establishes that the free parameter fraction $ρ_f$ is topological invariant, a provable compression ceiling computable in $\mathcal{O}(|V|+|E|)$; (2) a \textbf{Two-Stage Hierarchical Search} that prunes candidate architectures by $L_1$-importance channel selection, ranks them by output fidelity as a zero-shot label-free proxy, and applies GhostConv structural mutation to Pareto-optimal candidates; and (3) the \textbf{first source-code-free compression pipeline for 3D point cloud models}, operating entirely via ONNX graph surgery with no original architecture definition required. On ModelNet40, H3DNAS reduces the number of parameters in PointNet, PointNet++, and PointMLP by $65.5\%$, $43.2\%$, and $49.1\%$, respectively, while achieving $1.99\times$, $1.29\times$, and $1.67\times$ inference speedups with negligible loss in accuracy. The source code is publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/ClarityLab-Org/h3dnas}.

</details>


### [76] [LoRA-TSD: Tangent-Space Spectral Descent for LoRA via Muon-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02734)
*Dmitrii Andriianov,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-TSD优化器，将LoRA的每一步视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，在切空间内执行Muon的谱范数最速下降步，并通过LoRA参数化原生的回缩（retraction）映射回因子。该方法避免了全权重矩阵的昂贵操作，回缩比截断SVD回缩快2.8倍，在六个基准上超越所有对比的LoRA优化器，并给出了首个全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA是微调大模型的标准方法，但其两个因子独立训练时，更新忽略了低秩权重变化所诱导的几何结构。现有方法忽略了低秩矩阵流形的内在几何，导致优化效率不高，且缺乏严格的收敛性分析。

Method: 提出LoRA-TSD优化器：将每个LoRA步视为固定秩矩阵流形的切向量，在切空间内执行Muon的谱范数最速下降步，再通过LoRA参数化原生的回缩映射回因子空间。回缩操作比截断SVD回缩更高效（快2.8倍），避免了全权重矩阵上的昂贵计算。证明了Frobenius范数版本的代理方法可还原为LoRA-Pro，并将切投影梯度（流形的黎曼梯度）识别为LoRA训练自然的平稳性度量。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-32B上，跨六个常识推理和自然语言推理基准，LoRA-TSD在所有对比的LoRA优化器中表现最佳，且对适配器秩的选择保持鲁棒。同时给出了LoRA-Pro和LoRA-TSD的首个全局收敛保证，收敛率使因子梯度范数趋于零。

Conclusion: LoRA-TSD通过将几何感知引入LoRA优化，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了微调性能，为流形方法提供了更便宜的回缩替代方案，并建立了坚实的理论收敛基础，是LoRA优化的有效新范式。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the standard way to fine-tune large models, yet when its two factors are trained independently, the update ignores the geometry of the low-rank weight change it induces. We introduce LoRA-TSD, an optimizer that treats every LoRA step as a tangent vector of the fixed-rank matrix manifold and takes the spectral-norm steepest-descent step of Muon inside that tangent space, mapping the result back to the factors through a retraction native to the LoRA parametrization. The step avoids expensive operations on full weight matrices, and its retraction is up to $2.8\times$ cheaper than the truncated-SVD retraction used by prior manifold methods. We prove that the Frobenius-norm version of our surrogate recovers LoRA-Pro, and we identify the tangent-projected gradient, the Riemannian gradient of the manifold, as the stationarity measure natural to LoRA training and computable from the factor gradients alone. Under this measure we give the first global convergence guarantees for both LoRA-Pro and LoRA-TSD, with rates that drive the factor-gradient norms to zero. Across six commonsense and natural-language-inference benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-32B, LoRA-TSD outperforms every competing LoRA optimizer and stays robust to the adapter rank. Code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/LoRA-TSD.

</details>


### [77] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Know Physics? Contamination, Units, and the Deterministic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02766)
*Wassim Tenachi,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault Levasseur,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨表格基础模型(TFMs)是否在训练过程中学到了物理学知识。通过在316个物理方程生成的数据集上评估四个TFM模型，对比六个基线模型，发现TFMs在物理数据上表现优异（开箱即用和调优后均如此），但其先验无法表示无噪声机制和物理单位，因此只能插值物理规律而不能作为真正的物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格是大多数物理测量数据的格式，表格基础模型以类似语言模型填充文本的方式学习填充表格，但尚不清楚这些模型是否在这个过程中学到了真正的物理知识。由于TFMs本质上是贝叶斯模型，关键问题在于它们的先验中包含了什么物理信息。

Method: 从316个物理方程中采样数据集，在域内和域外场景下，评估四个表格基础模型（TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT和Real-TabPFN-2.5）与六个基线模型的性能。通过直接探测模型先验来检验其对无噪声机制和物理单位的表征能力。

Result: TFMs在物理方程数据集上显著优于基线模型，且开箱即用和调优后的性能都很好。然而，实验表明TFMs的先验无法表示无噪声机制，也无法表示物理单位，这意味着它们虽然能有效插值物理数据，但尚不具备作为物理模型的能力。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型在物理数据上表现强大，但仅限于插值层面的"物理直觉"，缺乏对无噪声物理机制和物理单位的深层先验表征，因此不能直接作为可解释、可外推的物理模型使用。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) learn to fill in tables the way language models fill in text, and tables are arguably the format in which most physical measurement arrives. Did they learn any physics in the process? They are Bayesian by construction, so the question is what their prior contains. We probe it directly, evaluating four of them (TabPFN-3, TabICLv2, TabDPT and Real-TabPFN-2.5) against six baselines on datasets sampled from 316 physical equations, in and out of domain. TFMs dominate, out of the box and after tuning. But we show that their prior can represent neither a noiseless mechanism nor physical units, which is why they interpolate physics without yet being able to act as physical models.

</details>


### [78] [Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02817)
*Peixuan Han,Runhui Wang,Ketan Ramaneti,Jie Hao,Gerald Friedland,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cliff，一种利用现成LLM教师识别推理过程中首个错误来进行奖励塑形的策略，将rollout分解为正确前缀和错误后缀，并转换为token级优势信号，在12个场景中比标准GRPO提升7%、比on-policy distillation提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR依赖粗粒度的结果奖励，难以对中间推理过程提供有效指导。现有的过程奖励建模和同分布蒸馏方法引入了额外约束（如依赖专门的奖励模型或假设师生推理模式一致）。作者观察到推理过程一旦出错，后续评估信息价值有限，因此希望利用更细粒度的监督信号来改进RLVR。

Method: Cliff利用现成的LLM作为教师，识别每次rollout中的首个错误位置。据此将rollout自然分解为正确的前缀和错误的后续部分，并将该信号转化为token级优势：对正确前缀赋予正优势，对错误后缀赋予负反馈。整个过程不需要额外训练奖励模型，也不限制师生推理模式的一致。

Result: 在12个不同场景的实验表明，Cliff一致性地提升了推理性能，在标准GRPO基础上提升7%，在on-policy蒸馏的基础上提升15%，即便使用能力一般的教师模型也能获得显著效果。此外还分析了ground truth在Cliff中的作用以及训练动态。

Conclusion: Cliff是一种简单、通用且有效的RLVR改进方法，通过提供更丰富的细粒度监督来提升大语言模型后训练的推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.

</details>


### [79] [UE5M3 FP4 Block Scaling for Stable Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02846)
*Robert Hu,Carlo Luschi,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更简单的稳定4比特浮点（FP4）预训练方案：将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对，仅对反向梯度应用选择性随机舍入，省略随机Hadamard变换和BF16最终层豁免。在近1900亿token上预训练Nemotron-H 8B模型，相比Transformer Engine配方取得更低的训练和验证损失，并提升21.2%的模型主体token吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定的FP4预训练具有挑战性，因为E2M1有效载荷只能表示很窄的数值范围。NVIDIA的Transformer Engine配方通过当前张量缩放、随机Hadamard变换和BF16最终层来解决此问题，但这些操作都增加了FP4矩阵乘法之外的计算开销。作者希望简化这一流程，减少额外工作。

Method: 采用E2M1有效载荷搭配无符号E5M3块缩放，利用其更宽的数值范围支持周期性张量缩放。配方仅对反向梯度应用选择性随机舍入，省略随机Hadamard变换，并且在所有符合条件的内部线性层中使用FP4（不保留BF16最终层）。

Result: 在近1900亿token上预训练Nemotron-H 8B模型：相比Transformer Engine配方，最终窗口训练损失更低；在各自量化推理策略下，验证损失（held-out负对数似然）更低；所有三个报告的量化推理下游聚合指标的点估计值更高。原生执行消融实验显示，同时移除RHT和BF16最终块豁免可将模型主体token吞吐量提升21.2%。

Conclusion: 端到端软件模拟的FP4预训练可以用更简单的配方实现，并优于现有方法，同时为原生支持E5M3块缩放提供了有力论据。

Abstract: Stable 4-bit floating-point (FP4) pretraining is difficult because the E2M1 payload represents only a narrow range of magnitudes. NVIDIA's Transformer Engine \nv{} recipe addresses this with current-tensor scaling, a randomized Hadamard transform (RHT), and bfloat16 (BF16) final layers, adding work outside the FP4 matrix multiplications. We instead pair E2M1 payloads with unsigned E5M3 (\ue{}) block scales. Their wider range permits periodic tensor scaling, while our recipe applies selective stochastic rounding to backward gradients, omits RHT, and uses FP4 in all eligible internal linears.
  We pretrain a Nemotron-H 8B model for nearly 190 billion tokens. Compared with Transformer Engine \nv{}, the proposed block-16 recipe finishes with lower final-window training loss and, under their respective quantized-inference policies, lower validation loss measured as held-out negative log-likelihood. Its quantized-inference downstream point estimates are also higher on all three reported aggregates. A native \nv{} execution ablation that jointly removes RHT and the BF16 final-block exemption increases measured model-body token throughput by 21.2\%. These results demonstrate end-to-end software-emulated \uefp{} pretraining with a simpler recipe and motivate native support for \ue{} block scaling.

</details>


### [80] [Post-Training Language Models for Gold-Medal Performance in Coding Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02849)
*Aleksander Ficek,Sean Narenthiran,Mehrzad Samadi,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端的竞赛编程专业化pipeline，结合大规模题目筛选、合成推理轨迹、SFT和RL训练，并引入GenCorrect测试时计算策略。在IOI 2025和IOI 2026上，该系统首次以超过最高分人类选手的成绩亮相，Nano-CC在IOI 2025达到468分（超过金牌线438.3），而专门的Ultra-CC系统在IOI 2026获得535.4/600分，超过金牌线361.12和人类最高分498.27。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性编程已成为检验大语言模型推理能力的关键基准，而IOI和ICPC等国际竞赛代表了最具挑战性的场景。作者希望探索LLM在这种极限推理环境下能达到何种水平，并推动AI在复杂算法竞赛中的能力边界。

Method: 构建了端到端的专业化pipeline：(1) 利用22,000道精选题目进行大规模问题整理；(2) 生成合成推理轨迹；(3) 分别对Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B)进行监督微调(SFT)+强化学习(RL)，对Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B)仅进行SFT；(4) 提出GenCorrect，一种反馈驱动的测试时计算策略，通过迭代生成、评估和精炼多样化解决方案来提升推理质量。

Result: IOI 2025上，Nano-CC从130分提升到291分（post-training后），使用GenCorrect后达468分，超过金牌线438.3；Ultra-CC达到502分。基于这些结果构建的竞赛专用Ultra-CC系统在IOI 2026前瞻性评估中取得535.4/600分，超过金牌线361.12和人类最高分498.27，成为首个在IOI题集上超越最高分人类选手的AI系统。

Conclusion: 这是首个在IOI竞赛环境中超越人类顶尖选手的AI系统，证明了结合大规模数据、SFT/RL和测试时计算策略的端到端pipeline能有效将LLM推向极限推理场景，为未来AI在高级别算法竞赛和复杂推理任务中的发展提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Competitive programming has become a key test of large language model reasoning, with international competitions such as IOI and ICPC representing its most challenging settings. We present an end-to-end specialization pipeline combining large-scale problem curation, synthetic reasoning traces, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). Using 22,000 curated problems, we train Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B) with SFT and RL and Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B) with SFT alone. We further introduce GenCorrect, a feedback-driven test-time compute strategy that iteratively generates, evaluates, and refines diverse solutions. On IOI 2025, Nano-CC improves from 130 points to 291 after post-training and to 468 with GenCorrect, exceeding the gold threshold of 438.3 while Ultra-CC reaches 502. Guided by these results, we develop a competition-specific Ultra-CC system and evaluate it prospectively during IOI 2026. Under the same time, internet-access, and submission constraints as human contestants, it scores 535.4 out of 600, exceeding both the gold threshold of 361.12 and the top human score of 498.27. To our knowledge, this is the first AI system to outscore the highest-scoring human contestant on an IOI problem set.

</details>


### [81] [The Implications of Linguistic Illegibility for LLM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02852)
*James Mickens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"语言不可读性"（linguistic illegibility）概念，指LLM外部输出的语言及其机械化提取的语言特征无法可靠反映其内部计算。由于LLM内部计算本质上是激活空间上的数学运算，与自然语言之间存在有损转换，因此依赖模型语言自我报告的安全机制（如思维链监控、宪法自我批评、激活探针）永远无法完全可靠。作者主张采用污点追踪（taint tracking）等不依赖语言解读的沙箱隔离技术，并结合稳健虚拟化、第三方审计等机制，为语言监控提供安全底线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据表明，LLM外部语言输出和机械化提取的语言特征可能不可靠地反映其内部计算。若安全机制依赖模型的语言自我报告，则可能被绕过。因此需要识别这一根本性局限，并探索不依赖语言解读的安全保障方案。

Method: 论文通过论证引入"语言不可读性"这一术语框架，分析LLM内部计算（激活空间的数学运算）与外部语言（有损转换结果）之间的本质差异，进而批判性审视依赖语言自我报告的安全机制，并提出基于污点追踪的沙箱方案及其他补充性隔离技术。

Result: 得出核心结论：语言不可读性对LLM而言无法避免，因此依赖语言自我报告的安全机制不可能完全可靠。污点追踪策略可从先验角度定义不被模型数据影响的系统状态，是构建有效沙箱的可行路径。稳健虚拟化与第三方审计等方法共同构成语言监控之外的关键安全底线，可减轻前沿模型的沙箱利用风险。

Conclusion: LLM安全机制必须超越语言层面的监控，采用不依赖解读模型语言状态的隔离技术（如污点追踪、稳健虚拟化等）作为安全保障的根本手段，语言监控只能作为辅助而非核心防线。

Abstract: LLMs are trained to generate natural language. However, various strands of evidence indicate that an LLM's externalized linguistic outputs and mechanistically-extracted linguistic features can be an unreliable lens for understanding internal model computation. We introduce the term ``linguistic illegibility'' to broadly refer to scenarios in which an LLM's externalized or mechanistically-probed language artifacts fail to represent how the model actually thinks. We argue that the specter of linguistic illegibility is unavoidable for LLMs whose internal computations are not directly expressed via language, but rather math over activation spaces (with lossy translations between activation spaces and natural language happening at the bookends). If linguistic illegibility is always possible, then security mechanisms that rely on a model's linguistic self-reporting (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, constitutional self-critique, activation probing for linguistically-defined feature vectors) can never be completely sound; the model sandbox will always need isolation techniques whose guarantees do not depend on reading a model's linguistic state at all. We argue that observing a model's outputs using taint tracking is a promising approach for an effective sandbox: regardless of how a model linguistically self-reports, a taint tracking policy can define, a priori, various pieces of system state that should never be influenced by model-produced data. We also discuss several additional sandboxing mechanisms (e.g., robust virtualization, third-party auditing of sandboxing configurations) which collectively provide a critical floor beneath linguistic monitoring, and would have mitigated recent sandbox exploits by frontier models.

</details>


### [82] [Graph Machine: Towards Better Pretraining via Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02881)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图机器（GM）架构，通过稀疏动态路由维护O(n)大小的状态，并用可微分更新的边（指针式对象）实现类指针追踪的引用机制，替代Transformer中75%的稠密层，在15.7B token上从头预训练Qwen3-0.6B，仅检索少量token即可维持甚至略微提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法的局限：固定大小状态无法扩展，稀疏但静态的路由将可访问状态限制在O(1)。作者希望设计一种既能保持稀疏层O(n)复杂度，又能访问O(n)规模动态状态的架构。

Method: GM架构维护O(n)大小的状态，通过稀疏动态路由访问。核心创新是使用"边"作为指针类对象，通过类似指针追逐的"推荐机制"（referral mechanism）进行可微分更新，从而在稀疏层中实现O(n)复杂度的同时保持O(n)状态的可访问性。

Result: 将Qwen3-0.6B中75%的稠密Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层，从零预训练15.7B token。每个KV头仅检索4096个token中的2个时损失仅轻微下降；检索4个时最佳模型损失略有改善。

Conclusion: GM证明了稀疏动态路由可以替代大部分稠密层而不显著损失性能，甚至检索更多token时可轻微超越全稠密基线，为高效稀疏Transformer架构提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Machine (GM), an architecture that maintains an $O(n)$-sized state and accesses it through sparse, dynamic routing. Unlike methods with fixed-size states or sparse but static routing, GM preserves $O(n)$ complexity in its sparse layers without restricting the potentially accessible state size to $O(1)$. Instead, GM uses edges - pointer-like objects updated differentiably by a referral mechanism resembling pointer chasing. We replace 75% of the dense Transformer layers in Qwen3-0.6B with GM sparse layers and pretrain from scratch on 15.7B tokens. With only 2 of 4,096 tokens retrieved per KV head in each sparse layer, loss degrades only slightly; with 4, the best model marginally improves loss.

</details>


### [83] [A Common Measure of Communication for Speech Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02887)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Benjamin Ballyk,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一种基于信息论的度量标准，用于在统一通信尺度上比较不同条件下的语音脑机接口系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音脑机接口领域缺乏统一的性能度量标准，因为不同系统使用不同的数据集、记录方法、语音类型和词汇表，导致报告的性能分数难以比较。核心问题在于：(1) 系统应使用户能够交流哪些词的分布；(2) 系统能传达该分布中的多少信息。

Method: 提出开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一种信息论量，测量解码器相对于用户可能希望交流的词的参考分布所传达的信息量。这使得在不同条件下（如不同词汇表）测量的能力可以在共同的通信尺度上评估。

Result: 表明常规报告的准确率、词错误率（WER）及其他仅基于系统支持词汇计算的指标可能高估系统传达用户意图语音的能力。使用OVMI比较现有系统，揭示了系统支持用户语言程度与解码准确性之间的权衡，并演示了最大化OVMI的词汇选择在三个语音域中相对准确率提升高达16.3%。

Conclusion: OVMI为语音脑机接口社区提供了一种有原则的方法来比较异构系统、改进词汇表设计，并衡量该领域的进展。

Abstract: Speech brain-computer interfaces (speech BCIs) translate neural activity into language, offering a path towards restoring speech for people with paralysis and, more broadly, enabling new forms of natural human-computer interaction. Despite this promise, the field lacks a common measure of progress because systems use different datasets, recording methods, types of speech, and vocabularies, so their reported scores are rarely comparable. Underlying this measurement problem are two unresolved questions: (i) what distribution of words should a speech BCI enable a user to communicate, and (ii) how much information from this distribution can a system convey. We address both by deriving open-vocabulary mutual information (OVMI), an information-theoretic quantity that measures the information conveyed by a decoder relative to a reference distribution over the words a user may wish to communicate. This allows capabilities measured under different conditions, such as distinct vocabularies, to be evaluated on a common communication scale. We show that ordinarily reported accuracy, word error rate (WER), and other metrics computed only over the words a system supports can overstate how much of a user's intended speech the system can communicate. We then use OVMI to compare existing systems, expose trade-offs between how much of the user's language a system supports and how accurately it decodes those words, show that these comparisons depend on what the user is expected to communicate, and demonstrate that selecting a vocabulary to maximise OVMI yields up to 16.3% relative improvement in accuracy across three speech domains. OVMI therefore provides the speech BCI community with a principled way to compare heterogeneous systems, improve vocabulary design, and measure progress in the field.

</details>

### [84] [VirSqueezer: Generating Realistic Deformations and Squeezing Dynamics in VR from Fine-Grained Squeezing Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01698)
*Qian Zhang,Xiaoming Chen,Xiaorui Ma,Haisheng Li,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VirSqueezer是一个用于VR内容创作中模拟精细虚拟挤压效果的框架，通过SenseGlove捕捉挤压控制信号，并结合3D高斯泼溅与扩散模型，同时生成局部形变（主效果）和破裂、溢出等复杂挤压动态（次效果），并通过接触面积估算、物理属性推断和物理响应模拟来确保视觉一致性和时间同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挤压是手部操作中最自然的形式之一，在VR内容创作中能实现局部形变和动态行为等独特视觉效果（如捏爆可乐罐、榨果汁），但现有的3D高斯泼溅方法和基于扩散的视频生成模型难以模拟精细的虚拟挤压效果。

Method: VirSqueezer框架利用SenseGlove捕捉精细的逐指弯曲挤压控制信号，为用户提供推断的阻力力反馈；通过估算物体接触区域、推断物理属性和模拟物理响应，计算指导生成模型的条件，从而实现视觉生成与模拟的视觉一致性和时间同步。

Result: 广泛的评估表明VirSqueezer能够再现真实的局部形变、生成令人信服的视觉动态，并在精细挤压控制中保持一致性。

Conclusion: VirSqueezer能够直接从连续、精细的挤压控制信号中生成物理上真实的视觉效果，填补了现有技术无法模拟精细虚拟挤压的空白。

Abstract: Squeezing is one of the most natural forms of hand manipulation, inherently involving fine-grained, temporally evolving, per-finger flexion. In VR content creation, squeezing plays a unique role in enabling particular visual effects such as localized deformations and dynamic behaviors, e.g., bursting a Coke can or juicing a fruit, thereby expanding the expressive possibilities of VR content. However, existing techniques, such as 3D Gaussian splatting-based methods and diffusion-based video generation models, are limited in their ability to simulate fine-grained virtual squeezing effects. We introduce VirSqueezer, a framework designed to generate both localized deformations (primary effects) and complex squeezing dynamics, such as rupture and overflow (secondary effects). VirSqueezer captures squeezing control signals using a SenseGlove and provides the user with inferred resistance force feedback during the squeezing process. By estimating object contact areas, inferring physical properties, and simulating physical responses, VirSqueezer computes conditions that guide generation models for visual effect generation, ensuring both visual coherence and temporal synchronization with the simulation. Consequently, VirSqueezer enables the generation of physically realistic visual effects directly from continuous, fine-grained squeezing control signals. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates VirSqueezer's ability to reproduce realistic localized deformations, generate convincing visual dynamics, and maintain consistency in fine-grained squeezing controls.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Instruction-Driven Editing: Source-Grounded Problem Discovery with User-Governed Repair for Scientific Posters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01813)
*Xingda Lyu,Honglin Lu,Xinye Luo,Shiqi Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PROS系统，用于弥合交互式编辑器中的"articulation gap"——用户发现工件有问题但不知道如何表达修改请求的早期交互状态。PROS将认知主动权与行为权威分离，系统可主动发现基于来源的问题，而用户决定修复目标与变更是否提交。同时提出PROS-Bench基准（120篇论文、320个可编辑PPTX海报），实验表明PROS在诊断质量与目标解决率方面表现良好，但部分指标的下滑揭示了问题发现、局部解决与实际结果需分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式编辑器假设用户已经知道自己要改什么，却忽略了更早的重要交互状态：用户能感知工件有问题但不知如何请求干预。本文将该状态定义为"articulation gap"，旨在解决智能编辑器如何在用户无法明确表达需求时主动支持问题发现，同时不剥夺用户对重大变更的决策权。

Method: 提出PROS系统，将认知主动权（系统发现并呈现基于来源的候选问题）与行为权威（用户决定哪些成为修复目标、变更是否提交）分离。接受的问题移交给原生PPTX对象编辑，并配备验证与可逆预览机制。同时构建PROS-Bench基准，包含120篇论文与320个可编辑PPTX海报，设有120张海报匹配的主核心集与独立的会议表示挑战集。

Result: 在主核心集上，PROS获得67.2（0-100量表）的VLM评分阶段平衡诊断质量分数，已接受诊断中87.6%的操作者验证目标解决率。时间盲自动化评分显示+22.7点的论文宏已接受目标提升，但14.8%的可评估已接受目标出现下降。

Conclusion: 实验结果的分歧表明，问题发现、局部解决与实际结果应分开评估。更广泛地，智能编辑器能够在用户提出具体编辑请求之前支持问题发现，而不需要对重大变更掌握全部权力。

Abstract: Interactive editors usually assume that users already know what to change. Yet an important interaction state comes earlier: a user may recognize that an artifact is not working without knowing what intervention to request. We call this the articulation gap. We introduce PROS (Proactive Refinement Of Scientific Posters), which separates epistemic initiative from behavioral authority: the system can surface source-grounded candidate problems, while users decide which become repair goals and whether resulting changes are committed. Accepted issues hand off to native-object PPTX editing with validation and reversible preview. We also introduce PROS-Bench, a source-linked collection of 120 papers and 320 editable PPTX posters, including a 120-poster matched primary core and a separate conference representation challenge. On the primary core, PROS achieves a mean VLM-rated stage-balanced diagnosis quality score of 67.2 on a 0-100 scale and 87.6% operator-verified target resolution among accepted diagnoses. Temporally blinded automated scoring yields a +22.7-point paper-macro accepted-target uplift, yet 14.8% of assessable accepted targets decline. This divergence shows why problem discovery, local resolution, and realized outcome should be evaluated separately.
