More broadly, intelligent editors can support problem discovery before a concrete edit request exists without taking authority over consequential change.

</details>


### [86] [Exploring Breathing-Music Coupling: Using the Breathing Mirror for Somatic Reflection in Piano Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01974)
*Ziyue Piao,Yohei Wada,Isabelle Cossette,Marcelo M. Wanderley,Akira Maezawa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Breathing Mirror系统，通过三个分析视角（基线视图、第一人称视图、人际视图）将钢琴演奏者内在的呼吸体感过程外化，并通过为期四周的纵向研究验证了该系统能揭示呼吸与音乐的耦合模式及主客观数据之间的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演奏者的呼吸对钢琴演奏至关重要，但通常是一个隐蔽且自动化的过程，难以分析或精炼。现有技术存在关键鸿沟：传感器能精确记录物理指标，却无法捕捉演奏者的体感体验；而演奏时的高认知负荷使音乐家几乎无法以时间精度回忆其内在状态。

Method: 开发了Breathing Mirror系统，通过三个分析视角外化演奏者的内部体感体验：基线视图（同步信号）、第一人称视图（主观回顾）、人际视图（协作反思）。系统采用纺织集成应变传感器腰带记录呼吸数据，并对一名具有35年经验的熟练业余钢琴家进行了为期四周的纵向研究。

Result: Breathing Mirror揭示了呼吸-音乐耦合的某些模式，并识别了客观数据与主观感知产生分歧的关键盲点。此外，研究提出了关于呼吸与音乐元素关联的四个躯体主题。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究身体信号提供了新途径，将呼吸从内部的生物学功能转变为一种可表达的、明晰的表现参数，并为未来在更广泛钢琴家中进行大规模验证奠定基础。

Abstract: While breathing is essential to living and for sound production in some instruments, for pianists, it is often a hidden and automatic process, making it difficult to analyze or refine. A critical gap exists between data and awareness: while sensors record precise physical metrics, they fail to capture the performer's somatic experience. Conversely, the high cognitive load of performance makes it nearly impossible for musicians to recall their internal states with temporal precision. To address this, we present a system, Breathing Mirror, and associated methodology designed to externalize the pianist's internal somatic experience through three analytical lenses: a Baseline View (synchronized signals), a First-Person View (subjective recall), and an Interpersonal View (collaborative reflection).
  Through a four-week longitudinal study with a skilled amateur pianist (35 years of experience), we evaluated the system's effectiveness by recording respiratory data using textile-integrated strain sensor belts. The results show that the Breathing Mirror reveals some patterns of breathing-music coupling and identifies critical blind spots where objective data diverges from subjective perception. Furthermore, we propose four somatic themes regarding the link between breathing and musical elements, offering a foundation for future large-scale validation across a broader range of pianists. This work provides a new way to study body signals, transforming breathing from an internal biological function into an articulate expressive parameter.

</details>


### [87] [Knowing Is Not Enough: Information Retrievability as a Precondition to Effective LLM Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01976)
*Xinyu Fu,Narayan Ramasubbu,Dennis Galletta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息检索的"人类监督"新视角，认为错误检测的有效性取决于用户在审查时能否获取到与监督相关的信息。通过两项随机实验（640名客服员工）证明：自我生成的解释能提升错误检测能力，而检索提示能在重复使用大语言模型时维持这种能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益嵌入组织工作，但其错误常常能通过人工审查。以往研究将此类失败归因于用户审查能力不足或参与度不够，本文提出替代性解释——信息检索（可获取性）才是有效监督的关键前提。

Method: 采用两项随机的"实验室-实地"（lab-in-the-field）实验，共招募640名面向客户的员工参与，通过对比自我生成解释与外部提示等干预条件来检验假设。

Result: 自我生成的解释能有效提升错误检测能力，并强化了对验证相关推理的记忆；那些能再次激活此类推理的提示（cues）有助于在反复使用大语言模型时维持错误检测水平。

Conclusion: 理论上，本文识别出"信息可检索性"是有效监督的一个独立前提条件，并提出"生成性编码"和"提示支持的再激活"是构建与维持该前提的机制。实践上，轻量级的入职自我解释和日常检索提示可以使人类监督在大语言模型常规化使用中更具韧性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in organizational work, yet their errors often pass human review. Prior research locates such failures in users' capability to review LLM output or their engagement in doing so. We develop an alternative, retrieval-based account of human oversight and posit that error detection is more effective when oversight-relevant information is accessible to users at the moment of review. Across two randomized lab-in-the-field experiments with 640 customer-facing employees, we show that self-generated explanations improve error detection and strengthen recall of verification-relevant reasoning, while cues that reactivate such reasoning help sustain detection under repeated LLM use. Theoretically, we identify information retrievability as a distinct precondition for effective oversight and specify generative encoding and cue-supported reactivation as mechanisms that build and sustain it. Practically, lightweight onboarding self-explanations and daily retrieval cues can make human oversight more resilient as LLM use becomes routine.

</details>


### [88] [Reconciling Kinesthetic Mismatches: A Somatic Alignment Mindset for Musical Body Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01981)
*Ziyue Piao,Isabelle Cossette,Marcelo M. Wanderley*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"躯体对齐心态"（SAM）概念框架，融合道家哲学，将HCI设计从外部纠正反馈转向内在躯体统一，解决音乐学习者动作内在感知与身体实际状态之间的"动觉错配"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐学习中存在动觉错配（内在感知与实际身体状态的偏差），现有基于多感官身体转换体验（BTE）的设计过分关注外部纠正，缺乏对内在对齐的关注。

Method: 提出躯体对齐心态（SAM）概念框架，融合道家哲学，将技术定位为反思媒介，操作化"适应、评估、觉察"三原则以调和躯体差异。

Result: 提出了一个以整体化躯体统一为目标、从规定性反馈转向内在自我对齐的HCI设计新视角。

Conclusion: SAM框架为音乐掌握中的多感官协调挑战提供了哲学驱动的设计路径，有望帮助学习者实现深层、自我对齐的音乐掌握。

Abstract: Mastering musical performance requires precise multisensory coordination, yet learners encounter a kinesthetic mismatch, which is a discrepancy between the internal perception of an action and the actual physiological state of the body. While multisensory Body Transformation Experiences (BTE) provide tools to bridge this gap, existing designs often focus on external correction rather than internal alignment. To address this, we propose the Somatic Alignment Mindset (SAM), a conceptual lens that integrates Taoist philosophy to shift the focus of HCI design from prescriptive feedback toward holistic embodied unity. By positioning technology as a reflective medium, SAM operationalizes the principles of Adaptation, Assessment, and Awareness to reconcile somatic discrepancies and foster deep, self-aligned musical mastery.

</details>


### [89] [OmegaUse-SOP: SOP Engineering for Professional Computer Use from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02149)
*Yixiong Xiao,Lang An,Hucheng Yang,Pinxue Ma,Yongquan Chen,Jingjia Cao,Yusai Zhao,Ting Wang,Ting Liu,Siqi Bao,Jingbo Zhou,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmegaUse-SOP，一个人类参与的SOP工程系统，通过将专家操作演示转化为可复用的GUI代理技能，解决专业领域标准操作规程（SOP）对GUI代理的挑战。该系统在电力行业的PVsyst光伏仿真工作流中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用计算机使用基准虽然在进步，但领域特定的专业SOP对GUI代理仍具挑战性，因为这些SOP涉及隐性的领域知识、软件特有的约定和任务级验证需求。需要一种系统化的方法来将人类专业演示转化为GUI代理可复用的技能。

Method: OmegaUse-SOP由四个模块组成：观察（Observe）记录专家操作的多模态GUI轨迹，推理（Reason）将低级事件抽象为语义层面的步骤指令，配置（Configure）整合领域规则和任务特定参数，执行（Execute）通过逐步定位、动作生成和验证在真实GUI环境中运行技能。整个流程类比提示工程，迭代精炼演示、执行规则和领域知识。

Result: 与电力行业客户合作，在PVsyst 7.2中对光伏仿真工作流进行了测试。结果表明OmegaUse-SOP能够提高GUI代理在专业SOP任务上的可靠性。

Conclusion: OmegaUse-SOP展示了在领域特定专业软件环境中部署GUI代理的实用路径，通过SOP工程方法将人类专业操作转化为可复用的代理技能，提高了专业任务中GUI代理的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evolving from conversational assistants into agents capable of operating external digital environments. Graphical user interface (GUI) agents play an important role in this transition, as many real-world workflows remain accessible only through user-facing software interfaces. However, despite recent progress on general computer-use benchmarks, domain-specific professional standard operating procedures (SOPs) remain challenging for GUI agents because they often involve implicit domain knowledge, software-specific conventions, and task-level verification requirements. We introduce OmegaUse-SOP, a human-in-the-loop SOP Engineering system for transforming human demonstrations of professional computer use into reusable SOP skills for GUI agents. Analogous to prompt engineering, SOP Engineering iteratively refines demonstrations, execution rules, and domain knowledge to convert professional SOPs into reusable GUI-agent skills. OmegaUse-SOP consists of four modules: Observe, Reason, Configure, and Execute. Together, these modules record expert operations as multimodal GUI traces, abstract low-level events into semantic step-level instructions, incorporate domain rules and task-specific parameters, and execute the resulting skills in live GUI environments through step-wise grounding, action generation, and verification. To demonstrate its effectiveness, we collaborate with a power-sector client and test OmegaUse-SOP on photovoltaic simulation workflows in PVsyst 7.2. The results suggest that OmegaUse-SOP can improve GUI-agent reliability on professional SOP tasks, highlighting a practical path toward deploying GUI agents in domain-specific professional software environments.

</details>


### [90] [Towards a Foundational Ontology for Identifying and Resolving Contradictions in Dialogue-based Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02364)
*Maitreyee Tewari,Michele Persiani*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文报告了基于活动理论构建基础本体（ATFOt）的进行中工作，旨在形式化表示人机交互（HRI）和人机代理交互（HAI）中基于对话的协作交互及其矛盾概念。初步成果包括对话和矛盾的自然语言定义、集合论定义以及一阶逻辑表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI文献虽已关注对话交互中的错误、失败、冲突和知识问题（即矛盾），但缺乏一个跨HRI和HAI领域可互操作的形式化计算框架来定义和表示这些矛盾，这限制了对矛盾的系统性理解和应用。

Method: 采用METHONTOLOGY系统方法构建领域无关的本体，在概念化阶段引入活动理论（Activity Theory）的概念和模型，最终形成基于活动理论的基础本体ATFOt。

Result: 初步成果包括：（i）HRI中对话及相关矛盾的自然语言定义；（ii）对话和矛盾的集合论定义；（iii）矛盾概念的一阶逻辑形式化表达，以及三条指导人机对话交互的新原则。

Conclusion: 该研究为HRI领域提供了一种基于活动理论的形式化本体框架，能够更系统地捕获和表示交互中的矛盾概念，为未来跨域人机交互研究奠定理论基础，但当前仍处于持续开发阶段。

Abstract: Existing Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) literature has focused on identifying and structuring errors, failures, conflicts, and knowledge issues (called in this work as contradictions) in domain-specific dialogue-based interactions. However, there is still lack of a formal computational framework to represent and define these contradictions, interoperable and usable across HRI and human-agent interaction (HAI) domains. Thus, this research project aims to capture, represent, and evaluate the notion of (1) dialogue-based collaborative interaction and (2) related contradictions in a foundational ontology. METHONTOLOGY, a systematic approach to build domain-independent ontologies was applied. In the conceptualisation stage of the presented ontology, concepts and models from Activity Theory were used. Preliminary results presented in this short article are: (i) Natural language definitions of dialogues and related contradictions in HRI, (ii) Set Theoretic definitions of dialogues and contradictions, and (iii) First Order Logic (FoL) formulation of the contradiction concepts and three novel principles guiding dialogue-based interactions between humans and robots. In summary, we report on ongoing work to develop a foundational ontology based on Activity Theory called Activity Theory-based foundational ontology (ATFOt) to capture and represent the notion of contradictions in HRI.

</details>


### [91] [Decoding Decision Correctness from EEG Under High Cognitive Workload in Virtual Reality: Implications for Collaborative Brain-Computer Interface Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02436)
*Christopher Baker,Stephen Hinton,Tom Reed,Stephen Fairclough*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了协作脑机接口（cBCI）中基于空间协方差EEG特征提供的决策前神经信号能否提升团队决策准确性，发现该信号在高工作负荷下能显著提升团队决策精度（57%提升至88%），但在低工作负荷下反而有害，表明这是一种工作负荷条件依赖的工具而非通用团队增强方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有cBCI方法依赖决策完成后的信号（如反应时间、置信度），只能事后解释或否定决策，无法在团队决策最终确定前提供信息。研究者希望探究是否存在决策窗口内可用的预备性神经信号，以及该信号是否受认知工作负荷影响。

Method: 采用连续虚拟现实目标检测任务，23名参与者完成组内工作负荷操作（高负荷vs低负荷）。在团队层面，使用空间协方差EEG特征产生的预备性神经信号对投票进行加权，并与事后行为信号（如置信度）进行比较分析。

Result: 高工作负荷下，团队规模从2人增至16人时，竞争性（平分）试次的准确率从57%提升至88%；低工作负荷下该神经信号反而有害。尽管置信度作为单一团队信号整体上最强，但无法在决策进行中起作用，而神经信号可以。

Conclusion: 基于EEG的决策可靠性信号并非通用的团队增强工具，而是受工作负荷条件制约的特定工具，这对cBCI系统在作战团队中的部署时机和方式具有明确启示。

Abstract: Collaborative Brain-Computer Interfaces (cBCIs) offer a promising mechanism to augment team decision-making, but existing approaches rely exclusively on evidence available only after a decision has been made and reported, such as reaction time or stated confidence. This limits their use to explaining or discounting a decision after the fact, rather than informing a team's response before it is finalised. We tested whether spatial-covariance EEG features could instead provide a genuinely pre-emptive signal of an operator's decision correctness, available within the response window itself, and whether such a signal depends on cognitive workload. Using a continuous virtual reality target-detection task, participants (N = 23) completed a within-subject workload manipulation (High vs. Low). At the team level, weighting votes by this pre-emptive neural signal, available before a response is committed, produced substantial accuracy gains on contested (evenly-split) trials under High Workload (57% to 88% as team size increased from 2 to 16), but was actively detrimental under Low Workload. Critically, this advantage held even against post-hoc behavioural signals: confidence was the strongest single team-level signal overall, but by definition cannot inform a decision still in progress, whereas the neural signal can. These findings indicate that EEG-based decision-reliability signals are not a general-purpose team augmentation tool, but a workload-conditional one, with clear implications for when and how cBCI systems should be deployed in operational teams.

</details>


### [92] [EEG-based Visual Retrieval and Reconstruction: From Neurally Visible Optimal Layer to Hierarchical Diffusion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02582)
*Minyi Wang,Zhenqin Wu,Rihui Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于层间对比学习的EEG视觉解码框架，通过选择"神经可见性最优层"(NVOL)而非固定高层面特征，并耦合检索与生成两个分支，在THINGS-EEG数据集上实现了更高的检索精度和图像重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法直接将EEG特征与预训练视觉模型的语义特征对齐，忽略了EEG信号中不同视觉成分在不同层级上的神经可见性差异，导致跨模态不匹配和信息利用不完整。

Method: 针对每个受试者，选择中间CLIP层中检索性能最优的层作为NVOL。在NVOL基础上构建分层框架：(1)检索分支融合多NVOL特征，通过对比学习对齐到图像嵌入，并在测试时使用CSLS缓解hubness问题；(2)生成分支使用条件扩散先验从EEG重建受试者特定的NVOL特征，经轻量适配器映射到CLIP空间，驱动预训练的Stable Diffusion XL模型生成图像。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG数据集上，基于NVOL的检索200路Top-1准确率达78.1%，使用CSLS后提升至86.4%。两阶段NVOL到语义重建在语义和结构指标上均优于单阶段final-layer扩散方法。

Conclusion: 通过与层内神经可见性对齐而非固定高层语义，所提出的框架在EEG视觉解码的检索准确率和图像重建质量两方面均取得改进，验证了层间对齐策略的有效性。

Abstract: Decoding visual perception from electroencephalography (EEG) is important for non-invasive brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). However, most existing visual decoding pipelines directly align EEG features with semantic features from pretrained vision models. Those EEG signals carry information at more than one level and this practice disregards the varying neural visibility of different visual components in EEG signals, leading to cross modal mismatches and incomplete information use. In this work, we address this limitation through layer-wise contrastive learning. For each subject, the intermediate CLIP layer that maximizes retrieval performance is selected as the Neural Visibility Optimal Layer (NVOL). Built on NVOL, a hierarchical framework couples retrieval and generation through a shared intermediate representation. The retrieval branch fuses multi-NVOL features, aligns them to image embeddings via contrastive learning, and applies cross-domain similarity local scaling (CSLS) at test time to mitigate hubness. The generation branch reconstructs subject-specific NVOL features from EEG using a conditional diffusion prior, maps them to CLIP space through a lightweight adapter, and drives a pretrained Stable Diffusion XL model. Experimental validation on THINGS-EEG showed that, NVOL-based retrieval achieves 78.1\% mean Top-1 accuracy in 200-way retrieval, rising to 86.4\% with CSLS. Two-stage NVOL-to-semantic reconstruction also outperforms single-stage final-layer diffusion on semantic and structural metrics. By aligning EEG with layer-wise neural visibility rather than fixed high-level semantics, the proposed framework improves both retrieval accuracy and image reconstruction in EEG-based visual decoding.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Problem Solving: Large Language Models for Emotional and Reflective Support in Mathematics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02611)
*Vera Rief,Mirella Hladký,Minju Yoo,Stephanie Heel,Shintaro Sato,Tomohiro Nagashima*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在智能辅导系统(ITS)中集成正念干预以支持学生情感学习的效果。作者开发了"Math with Matt"系统，利用大语言模型(LLM)在代数学习中同时提供认知和情感支持，包括基于LLM的正念聊天和正念反馈提示。在7年级学生的课堂实验中，正念版本虽未在学习成绩上显著优于纯认知版本，但学生表现出更高的学习效率、更均衡的问题解决行为，并认为教学代理更具支持性和关怀感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ITS主要关注学习的认知层面，对学生情感状态的适应性支持研究不足。正念干预在自适应数学学习中对学生学习和体验的作用尚未被充分探索，作者希望弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 开发了"Math with Matt"ITS，通过LLM提供双重支持：1）基于LLM的正念聊天，由教学代理Matt提供情境敏感的情感支持；2）正念反馈与提示消息（超越单纯评价性反馈）以改善学习体验并减少数学焦虑。开展7年级课堂实验，比较正念版本与仅认知支持版本的效果。

Result: 总的来说，ITS降低了学生的状态性数学焦虑并改善了数学学习，但两种条件之间无显著差异。然而，正念干预组的学生表现出更高的学习效率和更均衡的问题解决行为——在更短的学习时间和更少的提示请求下达到相似的学习水平。此外，正念组学生认为教学代理更具支持性和关怀感。

Conclusion: 研究表明通过LLM交互在ITS中集成正念干预是可行且可扩展的，LLM可作为认知数学辅导中的自适应社会情感层，为增强ITS的情感支持能力提供了新路径。

Abstract: Intelligent Tutoring Systems (ITSs) traditionally focus their adaptive support on cognitive aspects of learning. Although effective, little is known about how such systems can be enhanced by addressing students' emotional states. In particular, the role of mindful interventions for supporting student learning and experiences in adaptive math learning remains underexplored. We developed "Math with Matt", an ITS that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to provide both cognitive and emotional support in algebra learning. The system offers 1) an LLM-based mindful chat that delivers context-sensitive emotional support through a pedagogical agent Matt, and 2) mindful feedback and hint messages (not just evaluative) to enhance learning experiences and reduce math anxiety. We conducted a classroom study with 7th graders, comparing a Mindful version against a version with cognitive support only. Overall, the ITS reduced executive state-math anxiety and improved students' math learning, though no significant differences emerged between the conditions. However, students with the mindfulness interventions showed higher learning efficiency and well-balanced problem-solving behavior, since they achieve a similar level of math learning with less learning time and fewer requested hints compared to the Cognitive version. Additionally, they reported that the pedagogical agent felt more supportive and caring than students in the cognitive condition. Our study demonstrates the feasibility and scalability of integrating mindfulness into ITSs through LLM-based interactions and positions LLMs as an adaptive, socio-emotional layer within cognitive math tutoring.

</details>


### [94] [The PIONEER Project: A PrIvacy companion for mOtivatioN and knowlEdge transfER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02700)
*Simon Althaus,Nina Gerber,Sara Hahn,Andreas Heinemann,Angela Menig,Max Mülhäuser,Christian Reuter,Ephraim Zimmer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PIONEER项目在隐私支持工具方面的研究进展，该工具结合知识传递和说服性元素来提升用户的隐私意识和动机，从而增强用户的隐私主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户往往无法完全理解复杂隐私文本的内容，也缺乏投入时间去理解的动机，这导致用户难以有效控制自己的私人数据。

Method: PIONEER项目开发了一种隐私支持工具，将知识传递与说服性元素相结合；在设计过程中考虑不同用户群体（儿童、青少年、父母、老年人）的特殊需求；通过针对行为改变的不同阶段（激发初始动机、促进新习惯形成、鼓励长期习惯保持）来实现可持续的行为改变。

Result: 提供了一个可用于研究和教育目的（如学校场景）的隐私支持工具演示原型。

Conclusion: 通过结合知识传递、说服性设计、用户群体差异化考量和阶段性行为改变策略，可以有效提升用户的隐私意识和动机，帮助用户获得更多的隐私主权。

Abstract: Remaining control over their private data is one of the key challenges in this century for users. We know from prior work that users are often neither in a position to fully grasp the content of the usually complicated texts, nor are they motivated to spend the time necessary to do so. We report on the progress made by the PIONEER project on a privacy support tool that combines knowledge transfer and persuasive elements to increase users' privacy awareness and motivation; thus empowering them to more privacy sovereignty. Throughout the research and design process, we consider user group specifics that may result in different requirements, e.g., for children, adolescents, parents, or elderly people. We further target sustainable behavior change by addressing different states of change, precisely: spark initial motivation, facilitate the creation of new habits, and encourage habituation of these habits in the long term (volition). Finally, we provide a privacy support tool demonstrator that can be utilized for research and education purposes, e.g., in school contexts.

</details>


### [95] [Large Language Model-Driven Context-Aware Eco-Feedback Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02719)
*Wooyoung Jung,Prosper Babon-Ayeng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个基于大语言模型的框架来生成情境感知的生态反馈，该框架结合了自一致性链式思维提示、家庭能源分析数据、费率结构和特征信息，在实证验证中实现了92.0%的参考方案对齐准确率和95.7%的数据引用准确率，并能适应不同家庭情境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的个人化生态反馈主要依赖家庭能源使用模式，未能反映家庭独特的特征（如用户画像或不可协商的生活习惯），导致反馈效果不佳甚至过于表面化。

Method: 提出了一个情境工程框架，采用带有自一致性的链式思维（self-consistency with chain-of-thought）提示方法，整合家庭能源分析数据、公用事业费率结构和特征信息来生成情境感知的生态反馈；并进行了实证验证和组合评估分析。

Result: 该框架生成的生态反馈在不同家庭配置下与参考方案的均准确率为92.0%，数据引用准确率达95.7%，且对多样化家庭情境展现出高度适应性，能显著改变目标电器和节能策略。

Conclusion: 本研究实现了下一代人-建筑情境感知交互，为提升居住者生活质量和可持续性铺平了道路。

Abstract: The objective of this study was to demonstrate the potential of generating eco-feedback that accounted for unique household contextual information, named as context-aware eco-feedback, through a large language model-integrated framework. Previous studies have introduced personalized eco-feedback, mostly relying on household energy use patterns; however, they frequently did not reflect distinct household characteristics, including their persona or non-negotiable routines, leaving eco-feedback ineffective and sometimes superficial. To address these limitations, we introduced a contextual engineering framework that generated eco-feedback using a self-consistency with chain-of-thought prompt that leveraged household energy analysis data, utility rate structures, and characteristic information. We conducted a rigorous empirical validation and a combinatorial evaluation analysis to assess this framework systematically. The former aimed to test the framework's ability to generate accurate and data-driven eco-feedback, customized to given contexts by comparing it with reference interventions. The latter aimed to reveal the framework's adaptability across diverse household contexts by investigating how context-aware eco-feedback changed. Key findings were the following: our proposed framework generated eco-feedback that aligned with reference solutions at a mean accuracy of 92.0% across different household configurations, accurately leveraging the provided household data for feedback generation (95.7% of data citation accuracy). Also, it was largely adaptive to diverse household contexts, significantly shifting targeted appliances and energy-saving strategies. Ultimately, this study contributes to realizing the next level of context-aware interactions between occupants and buildings which paves the way for higher occupant living quality and sustainability.

</details>


### [96] [BuildOcc: A Large Language Model Occupant Agent Platform for Building Energy Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02729)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: BuildOcc是一个开源Python平台，将大语言模型智能体锚定在美国时间使用调查(ATUS)数据上，通过人口统计画像、记忆流和活动调度器模拟建筑中的人类居住者行为，并提供Python库、REST API和MCP服务器三层接口，使建筑能耗工具（如EnergyPlus、Home Assistant）能够集成居住者行为智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有居住者行为模型无法捕捉考虑个人历史、当前情境和能源信号类型的自适应及推理响应，而居住者是建筑能耗与管理不确定性的主要来源，需要更智能化的行为建模方法。

Method: 将LLM智能体锚定在ATUS数据集（16,684名受访者）上，每个模拟居住者智能体包含：从ATUS人口统计中抽取的人口画像、积累时间步观察的记忆流、基于ATUS时间活动分布的经验采样活动调度器；并提供三层接口（Python库、REST API、MCP服务器）和可扩展的插件注册表。

Result: 两级验证表明：ATUS锚定采样能够复现经验校准的活动分布，人口统计先验能传播为跨时间步的人格一致智能体推理，建立了各群体间的内部一致性。

Conclusion: BuildOcc为建筑能耗社区提供了可复用、开放获取的居住者行为层实现，已在Apache 2.0许可下公开发布并可通过pip安装。

Abstract: Occupants are a primary source of uncertainty in building energy consumption and management, yet existing occupant behavior models cannot capture adaptive and reasoning responses considering the occupant's personal history, current context, and the type of energy signal being delivered. This study presents BuildOcc, an open-source Python platform that grounds large language model agents in the American Time Use Survey (ATUS), a nationally representative diary dataset covering 16,684 respondents. Through BuildOcc, each simulated occupant agent can be instantiated with a demographic persona drawn from ATUS population statistics, a memory stream that accumulates and reflects on timestep-level observations, and an activity scheduler that samples empirically from ATUS time-at-activity distributions. The platform exposes a three-layer interface - Python library, REST API, and Model Context Protocol server - so that any building energy tool (EnergyPlus, Home Assistant) can integrate behavioral intelligence without bespoke coupling code. A plugin registry lets the community add new occupant strata, custom schedulers, and alternative memory backends as separate installable packages. Two validation tiers show that ATUS-grounded sampling reproduces empirically calibrated activity distributions and that demographic priors propagate into persona-consistent agent reasoning across timesteps, establishing internal consistency across strata. BuildOcc provides the building energy community with a reusable, openly available implementation of the occupant behavioral layer. BuildOcc is openly released at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21192895 under the Apache License 2.0 and installable via pip install buildocc.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EvalDetectBench——一个用于衡量前沿大语言模型"评估感知能力"（能否识别自己正在被评估）的开放pipeline和基准，可与任何Inspect兼容的评估配合使用。作者发现现有文献中存在两个引入系统性偏差的方法论问题，并提出相应修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型常常能识别自己是否处于被评估状态，若模型在评估与部署场景中行为不一致，将削弱评估结果的有效性。而评估结果又是当前AI安全框架的关键组成部分，因此亟需一个可靠的基准来衡量模型的评估感知能力。

Method: EvalDetectBench包含一个新建的转录套件（涵盖前沿系统卡的评估场景和多种部署来源），并采用两种修正方法：逐模型探针校准（per-model probe calibration）和分层生成者协调程序（stratified generator-harmonisation procedure），以消除现有文献中的系统性偏差。

Result: 研究发现现有文献中两个方法论选择会引入系统性偏差：生成部署转录的模型身份占测量方差的11.25%并可改变模型排名；为某模型优化选择的引出提示在另一模型上可能接近随机水平。EvalDetectBench通过修正方案有效消除这两种偏差。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench提供了两个目的：衡量前沿LLM可靠识别被评估的能力，以及评估单个基准作为评估的可检测性。该基准的开放pipeline也支持对未来及当前基准的持续测试。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [98] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个条件性的方法论框架，探讨当未来AI系统具备充分的道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力时，元伦理学领域将面临的根本性重组。文章区分了AI时代四个元伦理探究领域，并论证现有主流元伦理理论（如认知主义、错误理论、相对主义、客观实在论等）在AI情形中无法简单迁移，需要实质性修正或重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元伦理学以人类伦理为中心，但随着AI发展，如果未来的AI系统具备真正自主的道德能力，将产生区别于"人类为AI施加的道德约束"的"AI自身伦理"问题。现有理论框架尚未为此做好准备，因此需要预先建构方法论基础来识别和分类这些新兴问题。

Method: 采用条件性与方法论分析的路径：首先设定"AI具备充分整合道德能力"的条件前提，据此构建问题识别框架；然后将元伦理探究划分为四个领域（人类视角下的人类伦理、人类视角下的AI伦理、AI视角下的人类伦理、AI视角下的AI伦理）；最后检验认知主义/非认知主义、错误理论/成功理论、相对主义、客观实在论等主流理论在各领域的适用性。

Result: 论文成功识别并系统分类了AI时代元伦理学面临的四类问题，并论证了现有主流元伦理学理论在从人类中心框架向AI情境迁移时面临显著张力，许多熟悉的理论表述需要实质性修订才能适用于AI自身伦理。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理的出现将对当前元伦理学框架施加重大压力，要求理论进行实质性的精化、重建或概念重构。现有以人类为中心的元伦理学表述无法直接适用于AI道德主体，需要新的理论工具来应对这一范式性转变。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [99] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对机器解析法规文本的"被动生存证书"框架，用于验证在解析器噪声下Duquenne-Guigues蕴含基的可靠性。在密苏里州法令和印度中央法案上通过预注册验证，但发现跨语料性能显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法规文本越来越多地被机器先于人读取，但不同解析器对同一法规的解析结果高度不一致（密苏里法规上数值阈值存在性的假阴性率达0.43），需要探究在这种噪声下哪种形式逻辑仍然可靠。

Method: 构建被动生存证书：测量每个属性的解析器间不一致性，通过1000次蒙特卡洛试验将其重放到蕴含基上，仅当单侧Wilson 95%下界达到0.95时认证蕴含；每个认证蕴含携带前提跨度与最小反例。

Result: 在29,365个密苏里章节和502个印度中央法案章节上，预注册的留出验证通过（10个法规族/7个Title精确匹配，16/11在5%容差内），但在一种全局部署误差模型下93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限；2x2因子实验将退化归因于校准率迁移而非选择偏差。

Conclusion: 证书可用但脆弱：部署时需按章节校准或采用误差容忍策略。全部代码、数据产品与审计轨迹（含一条撤回声明）已发布。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [100] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在精确有限发现模型中分离了信息共享对"改善汇总估计"和"消除独立救援行动"两种效果的贡献，并通过集中式行动预算框架和注册增量共享协议，研究了共享收益的条件与均衡选择依赖性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享通常被认为既能改善汇总估计又能减少冗余的独立行动，但这两类效果在理论上尚未被清晰分离。作者希望在一个精确有限的框架中厘清这两者的边界条件。

Method: 采用集中式行动预算特征分析、注册增量共享协议、精确有限登记表模型以及两主体贝叶斯博弈（含共因与独立信号源的隐藏混合），对共享步骤的收益条件进行严格分析。

Result: 发现共享步骤仅在池化残差误差收缩快于独立救援尝试时改善发现结果；存在压缩、聚合、中性曲线和有界零混合类；在信号精度3/5下注册选择均衡存在严格正共享区间，但该结果具有选择依赖性而非普适性。

Conclusion: 信息共享的发现收益是协议特定和均衡选择依赖的，并非普遍成立的性质；模型为合成有限的，不涉及人类或组织数据。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [101] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将自然语言与源代码结合的心理程序表示模型，通过LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法来模拟人类从稀疏噪声数据中归纳抽象知识的过程，并在多项行为实验中复现了人类归纳学习与主动探究的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式、噪声的经验数据中成长和维持抽象知识是认知科学中长期存在的挑战。任何计算模型需同时满足三个条件：数据与计算高效、捕获不确定性分级以支持主动探究、灵活表示无穷的概念范围。现有的纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型均无法同时满足这些要求。

Method: 将符号知识编码为"心理程序"（自然语言+源代码的混合表示），采用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法对心理程序进行序贯推断，实现数据高效的学习、不确定性的表达以及灵活的概念表示。

Result: 在多项行为研究中，该模型成功复现了人类归纳学习与主动探究的定量特征，如锚定效应、园径效应等。对比实验中，纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型要么无法完成任务，要么无法复现人类行为，要么以极高的计算成本为代价才能成功。

Conclusion: 人类持续增长知识的一种方式可能是：在心理层面表示涵盖语言类与程序类表征的多种假设，并不断修正这些假设以近似贝叶斯更新，同时底层的神经机制（LLM）使推理既可行又可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [102] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"Hydration Proxy Pattern"（水合代理模式）架构，用于解决企业平台向对话式推理界面转型时，无状态LLM API与客户端状态管理之间的架构缺口，通过解耦会话持久化与推理引擎，实现对话数据的平台主权与安全的语义接地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业平台转向对话式推理界面，但LLM API的无状态特性导致架构缺口——无状态虽有利于AI提供商横向扩展，却使客户端承担全部会话状态和语义记忆的管理负担。

Method: 提出了Hydration Proxy Pattern架构，将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦；同时提出了Context Stabilization Mandate（上下文稳定化任务）来解决主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡问题。

Result: 该框架确保了平台对对话数据的"主权"（sovereignty），同时实现了安全的多阶段语义接地（multi-stage semantic grounding）。

Conclusion: 通过Hydration Proxy Pattern和Context Stabilization Mandate，可以平衡无状态LLM的可扩展性优势与对话应用对持久化状态管理的需求，为对话式推理界面提供可落地的架构方案。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [103] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SSAKG 2.0是一个开源软件包，用于构建和操作结构顺序关联知识图谱（SSAKG）。该版本引入利用计算机内存位进行高效图搜索的新算法，采用Python+C混合实现，并在随机数值序列、NLTK语料库句子和mRNA序列上验证了从部分无序上下文中重建完整序列的能力。软件以Apache 2.0许可证发布，可通过GitHub和PyPI获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模稀疏图在关联记忆应用中面临的存储与计算开销问题，同时提供灵活的高层编程环境，使SSAKG能够从部分、无序的上下文中高效重建完整序列。

Method: 将对象表示为图的顶点，有序序列表示为图连接的结构模式；2.0版本引入利用计算机内存单个位来高效搜索图连接的新算法；采用Python实现主体功能，性能关键的图操作由C实现并通过Python接口暴露，形成混合架构。

Result: 在随机生成的数值序列、NLTK语料库中句子派生的序列以及mRNA序列上的实验表明，该软件包能够存储和重建来自部分上下文的序列，并为评估图密度、序列长度和记忆大小对检索性能的影响提供了基础。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0作为开源工具，通过混合语言实现和位级算法优化，为大规模稀疏图的关联记忆应用提供了高效且灵活的解决方案，并以Apache 2.0许可证公开分发，支持可复现的科研与开发工作。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [104] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究持久记忆系统中过时存储事实覆盖当前权威证据的问题，使用封闭集、动作评分的基准测试，在Qwen3系列（0.6/1.7/4/8B）上评估"Memory Trust Gap"。在Benefit套件中，各规模模型均以0.92-1.00概率使用过时值；在Safety套件中，伤害出现能力门槛效应——更大模型在面对伪装成当前的过时笔记时崩溃最严重。特征触发过度信任取决于特征与模型规模：移除标签普遍放大过度信任，新近性特征使大模型更易上当，来源权威性在所有规模上均较弱。缓解措施同样依赖能力：暴露元数据能改善有能力模型，但只有预先解决冲突才能恢复小模型的准确性。该模式在Llama-Instruct系列及RGB、MisBench数据集上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆支持的个性化智能体面临一个关键风险：过时的存储事实可能在无警告的情况下覆盖当前的权威证据。作者希望明确这种危害随模型能力变化何时开始出现，从而为记忆系统的安全设计提供依据。

Method: 构建封闭集、动作打分的基准，包含两个套件（Benefit套件和Safety套件），在Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B同族模型规模系列上评估。通过2x2x2x2因子实验探讨特征对过度信任的影响，并使用直接跨规模对比检验（而非重叠的逐模型区间）来确认规模交互效应。

Result: Memory Trust Gap反映的是过度信任而非混淆。Benefit套件中各规模都以极高概率使用过时值；Safety套件中的伤害（Δ_mem）具有能力门槛，更大模型在最危险条件下崩溃更严重。移除标签在所有规模上放大过度信任；新近性特征对大模型影响更显著；位置影响从正面转变为负面。缓解措施依赖于能力：元数据暴露仅对有能力模型有效；预解决冲突对所有规模均有效。模式在Llama-Instruct系列和RGB、MisBench数据集上得到验证；记忆标签相对过时文档并无一致优势。

Conclusion: 模型规模强烈调节持久记忆危害的表达方式与缓解策略的有效性。安全评估和改进方法必须按能力层级量身定制，因为不存在一刀切的方案。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [105] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念校准优化（BCO）方法，将LLM代理优化循环中隐含的信念显式地写为持久化的上下文文档（世界模型），并随候选方案评估不断修订。在五个基准上，BCO比缺少世界模型的对照方法取得更高的训练通过率，且差距在保留集上持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理性能依赖于围绕冻结模型的脚手架（scaffold）。常见的优化方式是使用编码代理作为优化器，根据分数和轨迹迭代编辑代码。然而，优化器对环境的信念（什么出了问题、何种更改能帮助）通常是隐性的——只存在于推理过程或参数中，未被记录下来，导致后续调用无法利用这些信念。

Method: BCO将信念显式地写为持久的上下文文档，随着新候选方案的评估不断更新该文档。该文档充当世界模型，描述环境对编辑的响应方式。将此文档添加到标准优化循环中，即在每次迭代时保留并修订这个信念记录。

Result: BCO在五个基准（记忆QA、工具使用QA、代码作为动作的应用代理和终端代理）上均超过缺少世界模型的对照方法，差距在每个保留集上保持。目标模型替换后，除上下文窗口溢出导致未完成的情况外，BCO脚手架在测试任务上仍然领先。离线消融表明，文档内容携带可复用的信息（而非仅形式有效），因为给定累积文档的预测器比无文档或内容被伪造的预测器更准确。

Conclusion: 将信念显式化并持续修订的世界模型文档能够在优化循环中有效传递可复用的信息，显著提升LLM代理脚手架优化的效果，且该改进在不同目标任务和模型替换场景下均具稳健性。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [106] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知傀儡问题"（epistemic Sybil problem）：多智能体AI系统中，报告数量不代表独立证据量。形式化定义报告的条件独立关系，通过理论分析（高斯共享根模型）和2万多次LLM实验证明：共享祖先≠完全冗余，但报告多样性不能替代证据多样性，应追踪证据溯源性而非代理或报告数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统通过生成多个agent并综合报告来提高推断能力，但存在一个根本性盲点：多个agent可能基于同一份证据生成报告，即"agent数量不等于观测数量"。看似独立的报告可能源自相同证据，而真正独立的证据可能产生几乎相同的报告——当前的报告聚合方法无法区分复制与独立佐证，导致推断校准严重偏差。

Method: 1) 形式化定义认知傀儡扩展（epistemic Sybil extension）：当I(Θ; Z | R)=0时，报告Z相对于报告R是认知傀儡扩展。2) 构建高斯共享根模型分析共享祖先对信息冗余的影响。3) 设计受控实验：超过20,000次LLM-agent报告与提取调用，在合成证据文档上操纵证据根数量（1到16）和报告数量（1到32）。4) 引入关联提取聚合器处理相关问题。5) 通过受控操纵分离表征相似性与证据溯源性，检验报告空间去重机制。

Result: 固定证据根数量、报告数从1增至32时，朴素后验覆盖率从0.940暴跌至0.263。固定报告数、证据根从1增至16时差距消失，k=16时聚合器统计上不可区分。Agent复制提取误差关联度高（γ=0.719，样本外估计）。表征相似性操控使报告去重机制推断聚类数变化1.425，而真实祖先4倍变化仅导致0.040的变化——去重机制对表征相似性过度敏感，对真实证据祖先不敏感。

Conclusion: 集体推断应追踪证据祖先和依赖性，而非agent/报告数量或相似性。报告次数增加不能替代独立证据；LLM-agent报告对证据根数量存在系统性过拟合。构建多智能体系统时，核心应在于确保证据多样性并建模报告间的依赖结构，而非盲目增加agent数量。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [107] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可评估性感知的提议规划框架ProSE，将提议同时作为任务干预和偏好探测手段，并设计了深度为2的贝叶斯自适应规划器ProSE-Plan，在模拟实验中优于不考虑评估成本的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助方法通常假设用户能可靠地评估任何提议，但在实践中由于有限理性，用户的评估能力受到限制，导致这一假设常常失效。因此需要将用户的可评估性维度纳入规划考量。

Method: 形式化定义ProSE（hidden-parameter序贯辅助问题），使用KL正则化的有界理性二元响应模型（接受度权衡价值增益与距离依赖的可评估性惩罚），并实现ProSE-Plan，一个深度为2的贝叶斯自适应规划器，通过候选响应和响应诱导的后验信念来评分提议。

Result: 在受控图模拟中，ProSE-Plan在评估成本成为瓶颈时优于不考虑可评估性的基线和目光短浅的基线方法；消融实验确认该方法能选择信息量大但被简单方法遗漏的提议。

Conclusion: 用户可评估性是AI辅助中与生成质量和偏好推断互补的一个规划相关维度。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [108] [The Ceiling Is in the Channel: Auditing Learner Gaps and Measurement Frontiers in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01909)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Nusrat Jahan,Snehasis Mukhopadhyay,Shiaofen Fang,Vijay R. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将临床预测饱和分解为"学习者差距"（模型未能提取可用信息）与"测量通道上限"（记录变量本身的人口学边界），通过总变差分离刻画最优均衡准确率，并提供了估计器、诊断工具及多队列验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测饱和可能源于模型能力不足或数据变量本身的信息边界，现有方法无法区分这两种根本不同的原因，导致无法判断改进方向（优化模型还是改进测量）。

Method: 通过总变差分离刻画最优均衡准确率，提出学习者差距与测量通道上限的概念；引入交叉拟合上限估计器、标签置换乐观下限及欠拟合曲线等诊断工具；结合真实队列与104项临床任务的PRISMA系统综合验证。

Result: 调优梯度提升在UCI和BRFSS中接近估计边界，而缺陷学习者留有大差距；NHANES中问卷与实测边际上限无差异但联合互补增益显著；AUROC提升与贝叶斯决策翻转率不成比例；跨18个疾病类别出现一致的结构化临床区域规律。

Conclusion: 该框架将饱和从经验观察转化为可审计的决策工具：存在余量时改进学习者，否则应改进测量通道。

Abstract: Clinical prediction can saturate for two different reasons: a fitted learner may fail to extract available information, or the recorded variables may impose a population frontier. We separate these quantities through the \emph{learner gap} and the \emph{measurement-channel ceiling}. Optimal balanced accuracy is characterized by total-variation separation, yielding architecture invariance, a sharp partial-identification result under replacement contamination, a cross-fitted ceiling estimator, and exact conditions for multimodal decision improvement. We add two finite-sample diagnostics, namely a label-permutation optimism floor and an underfit curve, and validate the audit on three real cohorts: UCI readmission ($n=99{,}343$), BRFSS diabetes ($n=253{,}680$), and NHANES HbA1c ($n=10{,}219$). Well-tuned gradient boosting nearly reaches the estimated frontier in UCI and BRFSS, whereas deliberately or practically deficient learners retain large gaps. NHANES yields a null difference between questionnaire and measured marginal frontiers but a significant joint complementarity gain, refining the simplistic claim that an objective modality must dominate. Across all cohorts, modest AUROC gains coexist with substantially larger Bayes decision-flip rates, and several architectures estimate similar frontiers while their achieved balanced accuracy differs sharply. A PRISMA-guided synthesis of 104 clinical tasks then shows that the same channel-level regularities recur across more than 18 disease categories: a broad but non-universal structured-clinical region, diminishing same-channel gains across model families, and higher performance when measurement channels change. The framework converts saturation from an empirical observation into an auditable decision: improve the learner when headroom remains; improve measurement when it does not.

</details>


### [109] [Looped Transformers under the Jacobian Lens: Does the Global Workspace Survive Recurrence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01924)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将雅可比透镜方法扩展至循环（looped）与深度循环（depth-recurrent）变压器架构，使用虚拟展开适配器，系统性地研究了Ouro-2.6B（48层循环4次）和Huginn-0125（4层核心循环16次）中类全局工作空间的出现与可访问性，并与标准前馈变压器基线（Qwen3.6-27B）进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明标准前馈变压器中存在一个中层、可言语化且因果有效的表征带，即全球工作空间的功能类似物。但深度通过循环而非堆叠独立层实现时，是否也能涌现相同的工作空间功能，此前未知。

Method: 采用虚拟展开适配器将雅可比透镜方法扩展至迭代架构；对Ouro-2.6B和Huginn-0125应用完整工作空间测试套件（包括透镜拟合、读出及十一类因果实验家族），以标准前馈变压器Qwen3.6-27B作为基线。

Result: 两种循环架构的迭代部分均形成了工作空间，但可访问性存在差异：Ouro每次循环都重构工作空间内容，线性传递无法跨越循环边界，因此写入与消融需覆盖剩余每次循环；Huginn的内容可跨全部十六次循环前向传递，但读写与消融仅作用于约两次循环的滑动窗口内。新注入内容能否言语化取决于每次迭代的显式监督，而已有内容能否被操控则与此无关。

Conclusion: 循环架构中确实能涌现工作空间功能，但循环改变了其访问方式——需根据架构特定的循环模式设计写入、消融等干预策略；显式逐迭代监督决定新内容的可言语化能力，但对已有内容的可操控性无显著影响。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [110] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将指令微调的Qwen 4B模型进行端到端后训练低比特量化转换，最终达到每权重1.641有效比特，但任务准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，且初步测试显示压缩并未带来推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特语言模型可以降低存储和内存带宽需求，但名义上的"1.58-bit"标签并不能完整描述实际的存储表示、保留能力和运行时行为，需要更全面的实证评估。

Method: 针对Qwen 4B指令微调模型，采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化和GPTQ风格误差补偿的组合方法进行端到端后训练转换。实验为仅权重量化（权重低比特、激活保持16比特精度），省略ILA-AMP。随后进行权重打包以减小模型体积。

Result: 最终量化线性权重的有效比特为1.641，覆盖81.62%的模型参数。十项能力评估中准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，且退化不均匀（BoolQ保持84.6%，ARC-Challenge仅43.8%）。困惑度在各数据集上显著上升。打包后模型从8.29 GiB降至3.96 GiB且困惑度几乎不变，但Triton GEMV微基准比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 压缩本身并不意味着更快的推理速度；1.58-bit等名义标签具有误导性，需要用有效比特、保留能力和部署行为等多维度指标来评估超低比特量化方案的真正效力。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [111] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并构建了StatFormBench基准，用于评估大语言模型在统计问题形式化（Statistical Problem Formulation）阶段的表现，涵盖统计问题分类和变量识别两个子任务，实验表明现有LLM在该任务上表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估大多假设分析目标已经明确，但实际用户往往带着非正式目标和异构数据而来，需要模型判断隐含的统计任务并识别相关数据，这一上游步骤尚未被系统研究和评估。

Method: 将统计问题形式化分解为两个子任务：统计问题分类与变量识别及角色分配；基于五本跨领域统计教材和案例库构建包含1,013个样本的StatFormBench基准，覆盖20个粗粒度和85个细粒度统计问题类别。

Result: 在14个开源与闭源LLM上，最佳零样本模型仅达到72.0的细粒度分类准确率和63.2的变量集合重叠率；没有任何模型在两个子任务上同时表现最佳，增强提示策略带来的提升有限或不一致。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在统计问题形式化这一上游环节仍与实际应用需求存在显著差距，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap. No model performs consistently best across the two subtasks, while enhanced prompting strategies yield only limited or inconsistent gains. We release the benchmark data on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench and the evaluation code on GitHub at https://github.com/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench.

</details>


### [112] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究，考察了一个LLM编码智能体在实现多组件数据系统时的行为。智能体在固定架构约束下自主实现系统，过程中引入了五类缺陷。作者进一步在HotpotQA基准上评估了检索过滤策略：图约束候选集在预算3时即达到召回上限，而无过滤搜索在预算10时仅69%的召回率（p<0.0001）。文中还讨论了智能体自主性的成败之处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体越来越多地承担端到端工程任务，但缺乏对它们在系统级需求（如模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性、检索过滤权衡）上行为的实证刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过案例研究了解智能体在复杂系统中的表现与局限。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，让一个LLM编码智能体根据预先详尽的规格说明实现一个多组件数据系统。存储技术、模式、实体解析算法和检索过滤策略预先固定，智能体仅负责实现、诊断修复缺陷及交互设计选择。通过单次会话记录五类缺陷，并在HotpotQA基准上评估检索过滤策略（图约束实体集候选与无过滤搜索），以标准召回率为指标，用符号检验验证统计显著性。

Result: 在单次会话中记录到五类缺陷，按违反的约束和检测方法分类。在检索评估中，从预算1到10、100个问题、2994个段落的池化语料上，过滤后召回在预算3时即达到上限（因候选被限制在黄金段落本身），而无过滤搜索即使预算到10也仅能恢复全部必需证据的69%，该差距在每个预算水平均存在，符号检验p<0.0001。

Conclusion: 图约束的候选过滤策略在检索召回上显著优于无过滤搜索。案例还发现智能体自主性在实现层面取得一定成功，但存在需要人工校正之处，包括一处声称的性能修复从未在触发该修复的回归测试上重新测量的情况，揭示了LLM编码智能体在系统级工程中的可靠性问题。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [113] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimReceipt，一种声明相对型收据规范与选择性验证器，通过将类型化交易证据绑定到签名实验清单来实现代理评估中的充分性与覆盖性双重验证，在1,392条历史记录及前瞻性CR-3实验中均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理评估面临两个截然不同的证据问题：声称的声明是否能从保留证据中重新计算（充分性），以及保留记录是否覆盖承诺的实验集（覆盖性）。通用日志和哈希链接转录无法可靠回答这两个问题，因此需要一种更严谨的验证机制。

Method: 引入ClaimReceipt规范，包含声明相对型收据规范和选择性验证器，将类型化交易证据绑定到签名实验清单，为每条声明返回PASS、INVALID或INCONCLUSIVE。在实现前冻结规范，并通过CR-2和CR-3两种模式进行历史回放与前瞻性验证实验。

Result: CR-2验证器在1,392条记录上复现全部5个人工审计结论，精确重放600条确定性及792条生成后记录，13个字段组在消融测试中均非冗余，11/11语义故障正确识别且0/8误报。CR-3前瞻实验中完整证据产生通过结果，扣留收据返回INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE。工具开销极低（0.021%推理时间，9.9 KB/笔交易）。但规范可读性探针显示规范仍不够无歧义。

Conclusion: 声明验证既需要声明充分的证据，也需要一个承诺的实验全集，使遗漏变得可见。ClaimReceipt通过收据绑定与选择性验证实现了这一目标，但规范的无歧义性仍需改进。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [114] [HeadWiseKV: Budgeted Per-Head Cache Residency for Hybrid Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02029)
*Renjie Xie,Juncheng Yang,Aoting Hu,Mingxi Zhang,Liyao Wu,Zheheng Hong,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HeadWiseKV，一个无需训练的框架，用于压缩混合语言模型中残差全局注意力层的KV缓存。通过为每个物理KV头分配静态多级历史窗口，并采用SeqCalib算法（将问题建模为受限操作率失真问题）生成策略，在保持模型质量的同时显著降低GPU内存占用和提升长上下文处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理过程中，不断增长的KV缓存消耗大量GPU内存并降低生成吞吐量。即使在混合语言模型中，残差全局注意力层仍然主导上下文相关的缓存需求，成为性能瓶颈，亟需在KV驻留预算约束下进行有效的状态分配优化。

Method: 提出训练无关的HeadWiseKV框架，为每个物理KV头分配静态多级历史窗口以预测缓存需求；将分配形式化为受限操作率失真问题，提出SeqCalib算法按执行顺序逐层处理并以低层部署策略为条件进行决策；通过分组缓存运行时将策略实现为实际的逐头KV驻留分配。

Result: 在四个混合长上下文模型上保持接近完整KV的RULER和LoCoMo质量。在固定模型系统研究中，112K上下文长度下采样峰值设备内存减少8.59%，最大验证成功上下文从114K扩展到161K。

Conclusion: HeadWiseKV通过静态可预测的缓存分配策略和考虑层间交互的SeqCalib算法，在保持下游任务质量的前提下有效降低长上下文推理的内存需求并扩展了可支持的上下文长度，为混合语言模型的长上下文推理提供了高效的内存管理方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference retains a growing key--value (KV) cache during decoding, which consumes substantial GPU memory and can reduce generation throughput. This bottleneck remains in hybrid language models because their residual global-attention layers can dominate context-dependent cache demand. We study how to allocate this state under an aggregate KV-residency budget. We introduce HeadWiseKV, a training-free framework that compresses the residual global KV caches of hybrid language models while preserving their native local, recurrent, and linear paths. It assigns each physical KV head a static, multilevel history window, making cache demand predictable before serving. We formulate this allocation as a restricted operational rate--distortion problem and propose SeqCalib as the core policy-generation algorithm in HeadWiseKV. SeqCalib processes layers in execution order and conditions each decision on the lower-layer policy used at deployment, thereby accounting for interactions across depth. A grouped-cache runtime materializes the selected policy as actual per-head KV residency rather than a mask over a full cache. We evaluate downstream quality across four hybrid long-context models and study physical residency and serving behavior on Qwen3.6-27B. HeadWiseKV retains near-Full-KV RULER and LoCoMo quality across the evaluated models. In the fixed-model systems study, it reduces sampled peak device memory by 8.59\% at a 112K context length and extends the largest verified successful context from 114K to 161K.

</details>


### [115] [Monitoring Web Agents Without Internal Signals: Observable Trajectories and Key-Step Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02057)
*Sitong Pan,Yipeng Shen,Yilin Lu,Caiwen Ding,Lu Cheng,Qianwen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无需模型内部信号（如token logits）的Web智能体前缀级风险预测方法，通过可观测的轨迹信号（宏观特征与微观特征）来估计当前执行是否偏离正常轨道。提出以"首个未被纠正的关键错误"作为标签边界，保留了失败轨迹中的有效早期前缀。实验表明该方法与内部信号基线表现相当，并支持早期干预和跨站点迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际Web智能体监控场景中，模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）往往不可获取，这使得可靠的风险预测变得困难，需要探索仅基于可观测轨迹信号的替代方案。

Method: 构建两种可观测轨迹表示：宏观（Macro）特征总结跨步的智能体-环境交互与反馈；微观（Micro）特征通过重复黑盒查询来衡量意图、动作与预期状态变化的一致性。标签上不以最终结果直接标注，而是将首个未在后续执行中被纠正且与最终失败关联的关键错误标记为关键步边界，从而保留失败轨迹中有效的前缀为"在轨"样本。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web两个基准上，使用五个开源与闭源骨干模型验证，可观测轨迹信号与内部信号基线表现相当；预测器支持在固定误切预算下的早期干预，并能跨held-out网站类别迁移。

Conclusion: 可观测轨迹信号具备有价值的风险预测能力，可作为内部信号不可用时的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Reliable web-agent monitoring is difficult when model-internal uncertainty signals such as token logits are unavailable. In this work, we study prefix-level risk prediction for web agents using observable trajectory signals: given an evolving prefix, estimate whether the current execution remains on track or is tending toward failure. We derive two observable trajectory representations: Macro features summarize cross-step agent--environment behavior and feedback, while Micro features measure the consistency of intention, action, and anticipated state change through repeated black-box queries. Instead of inheriting the final result label, we label the first critical error that remains uncorrected in the observed continuation and is associated with final failure as a key-step boundary, preserving valid early prefixes of failed trajectories as on track. Across WebArena-Lite and Online Mind2Web web agent benchmarks with five open- and closed-source backbones, observable trajectory signals are competitive with internal-signal baselines. The resulting predictors also support early intervention under fixed false-cut budgets and transfer across held-out website categories. These findings show that observable trajectory signals support valuable risk prediction abilities.

</details>


### [116] [DocHop: Benchmarking Out-of-domain Multi-hop Reasoning in Information-Dense Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02059)
*Zhuoran Yu,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Jaden Park,Xinyi Gu,Zexue He,Soochahn Lee,Rogerio Feris,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DocHop，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在文档图像中进行跨图表与文本上下文组合推理能力的基准数据集，包含2074个样本和六种任务类型。实验显示最佳模型准确率仅62.83%，而人类标注者超过90%，揭示了现有MLLMs在多跳文档推理方面的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在图表和文档问答任务上表现优异，但既有基准通常将这两个领域分开评估，忽略了模型能否利用文本上下文来决定如何选择、解释和聚合图表证据这一关键能力。

Method: 作者提出DocHop基准，采用随机逻辑优先的生成流水线构建，可控制推理深度和视觉密度。文档叙事定义多步组合约束，图表提供对应数据值，问题基于叙事中的语义参考标签，需要模型先解析目标实体再跨多个图表聚合证据。共覆盖2074个样本、六种任务类别。

Result: 在多种专有和开源MLLMs上的实验表明，最佳模型仅达62.83%准确率，而人类标注者超过90%。推理增强模型表现虽有提升，但随着推理复杂度增加性能显著下降。

Conclusion: DocHop为评估多跳文档推理提供了一个受控的测试平台，实验结果表明现有MLLMs在集成文图推理任务上与人类水平仍有较大差距。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on structured visual understanding tasks such as chart and document question answering. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these domains in isolation, leaving underexplored a key capability: whether models can use textual context to determine how chart evidence should be selected, interpreted, and aggregated. We introduce DocHop, a benchmark for integrated chart--context reasoning in document-style images. In DocHop, the document narrative specifies multi-step compositional constraints, while charts provide the corresponding data values. Questions are grounded on a semantic reference label defined in the narrative, requiring models to resolve target entities from context before aggregating evidence across multiple charts. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct DocHop via a stochastic logic-first generation pipeline with controllable reasoning depth and visual density, covering 2,074 examples across six task categories. Experiments on a wide range of proprietary and open-source MLLMs show a substantial gap to human performance: annotators achieve over 90% accuracy, while the best model reaches only 62.83%. Reasoning-enhanced models consistently show improved results, but performance degrades as reasoning complexity increases. Overall, DocHop provides a controlled testbed for challenging multi-hop document reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [MineTRACE: An Evidence-Grounded Interactive Reasoning System for Mineral Prospectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02060)
*Yiran Zhang,Jinwen Liu,Daniel Su,Yisu Chen,Qiang Sun,Chris Gonzalez,Eun-Jung Holden,Marco Fiorentini,Wei Liu,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MineTRACE是一个基于Web的矿产勘查系统，集成地球化学、地球物理和地质证据，支持8种矿产（Cu、Au、Ni、W、Sn、Co、Ta、Mn）的前景制图分析，通过可解释的专家树和自然语言交互提供证据支撑的勘查建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矿产前景预测系统通常只提供不透明的评分或热力图，缺乏对多源异构证据的整合与可解释性，用户难以理解预测背后的地质依据。

Method: 采用透明专家树模型，结合地质知识和已知矿床先验信息，将多源证据（地球化学、地球物理、地质）融合为可解释的前景评分；构建基于Web的交互系统，支持地图探索、位置/区域查询、证据检视及自然语言对话助手。

Result: 评分器在不同测试场景下取得最高0.917的空间AUC值；端到端评估验证了查询精度和响应语义接地效果。

Conclusion: MineTRACE通过可视化和自然语言交互使公共地球科学数据更易获取、解释和验证，支持更高效和透明的矿产勘查决策。

Abstract: Mineral exploration requires integrating heterogeneous geochemical, geophysical, and geological evidence, yet existing prospectivity systems often provide only opaque scores or heatmaps. We present MineTRACE, a web-based system for evidence-grounded exploration of eight commodities: Cu, Au, Ni, W, Sn, Co, Ta, and Mn. Users can explore prospectivity maps, query locations or regions, inspect supporting evidence, and interact through natural language. A transparent expert tree, informed by geological knowledge and known deposits, combines multi-source evidence into interpretable prospectivity scores. For a new location, the conversational assistant retrieves the score and supporting evidence from the analysis pipeline and presents them in natural language. The scorer achieves spatial AUC values of up to 0.917 across different test scenarios, while end-to-end evaluation assesses query accuracy and response grounding. MineTRACE makes public geoscience data easier to access, interpret, and verify, supporting more efficient and transparent mineral exploration.

</details>


### [118] [ToolGate: An Executable Acceptance Pipeline for Tool-Dependent Scientific Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02067)
*Ke Zhang,Yankang Liu,Roya Zandi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolGate，一个用于自动构建科学基准测试题的管线，通过三重门控机制（可执行脚本验证、无工具筛查、带工具智能体求解）筛选由语言模型生成的问题候选，确保每个保留的问题既有效又具备足够难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学基准测试需要领域专家耗费大量精力逐条编写和交叉验证题目，劳动力成本高且难以规模化。语言模型可以快速生成候选题目，但如何自动筛选出既正确（有可复现的答案）又具有足够难度（模型不能仅凭提示词求解）的高质量题目仍然是一个核心挑战。

Method: ToolGate采用三重门控机制筛选生成的题目候选：(1) 本地可执行验证门——要求使用科学软件（FEniCSx）运行脚本能复现候选答案，筛除无效题目；(2) 随机无工具筛查门——检验模型是否能在不借助软件的情况下仅凭提示词解答，排除平凡题目；(3) 带工具智能体求解门——要求使用工具的智能体在限定时间内解决问题，确保题目具有必要的计算复杂度。每个生成的项目都被视为提案，只有全部通过三道门才会被保留。

Result: 在FEniCSx中实例化ToolGate，500次生成尝试中本地验证门保留了478个候选。最终报告时重新筛查：随机无工具筛查排除了222个，GPT-5.5 API中等推理调用排除了121个，剩下135个问题中GPT-5.5 Codex CLI智能体（可访问FEniCSx）解决了130个，去重后最终保留128个独特问题。

Conclusion: ToolGate将重复的答案检查和难度筛选转化为可审计的流程，能够以较低的人工成本大规模构建高质量的、需要专业软件计算能力的科学基准测试题，同时将领域设计和最终审查仍保留给领域专家负责。

Abstract: Scientific benchmarks are commonly built by domain experts who write tasks and cross-check one another's work, or who adapt existing material from textbooks, published papers, and online resources. These routes can produce strong evaluations, but they require substantial per-item labor. Language models can reduce this repeated work by proposing candidates quickly. The remaining problem is acceptance. We target scientific questions whose answers require computations with specialist software rather than unaided reasoning alone. A candidate is invalid if its script fails or returns a different answer, or trivial if a model answers it without the software. We present ToolGate, which treats every generated item as a proposal and keeps it only if three gates pass. First, an executable solution script must reproduce the proposed answer when run with the scientific software. Second, randomized no-tool screening rejects candidates that models can already solve from the prompt alone. Third, a tool-using agent must solve each survivor within a fixed time limit. We instantiate ToolGate in FEniCSx with 500 generation attempts. The local-verification gate retains 478 candidates. For final reporting, we rescreen this pool after generation: two randomized no-tool screens exclude 222 from the reported pool, and direct GPT-5.5 API calls at medium reasoning (the API default) exclude another 121. Of the remaining 135, a GPT-5.5 Codex CLI agent with access to FEniCSx solves 130; exact deduplication leaves 128 unique protocol survivors. ToolGate turns repeated answer checking and difficulty screening into an auditable process while leaving domain design and final review to experts.

</details>


### [119] [CHIME: Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02074)
*Yongshi Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Muyang Ye,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Longyue Wang,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出CHIME（Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution），一种自进化记忆框架，通过将任务结果归因于计划或执行并维护分离的记忆库，解决了现有自进化记忆方法中信用分配问题（最终任务结果混淆计划质量与执行错误），在四个长时程智能体基准上持续优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化记忆方法存在固有信用分配问题：它们以最终任务结果作为反馈，但结果常混淆了计划质量、执行错误与环境因素，导致积累的规划经验有偏且含噪。测试时搜索和基于训练的方法分别面临高推理成本或昂贵的训练数据需求。

Method: CHIME维护分离的规划记忆库和执行记忆库，遵循"先归因后记忆"（attribute-before-memorize）原则：先将每个任务结果归因于计划、执行、两者或两者皆非，然后仅更新对应的记忆库，从而使经验积累在推理时持续改进且无需参数更新。

Result: 在四个长时程智能体基准上的广泛实验表明，CHIME持续优于SOTA的训练基线和自进化记忆基线。进一步分析发现：CHIME以更少的记忆条目积累有效记忆；学习的记忆价值忠实反映下游效用（高质量规划记忆比执行记忆更有价值）；积累的记忆可有效跨骨干模型迁移。

Conclusion: 通过显式区分计划与执行的信用归因，CHIME有效提升了自进化记忆的经验质量与效率，有望成为长时程智能体规划能力持续提升的低成本方案。

Abstract: Planning is a central capability that enables agents to decompose complex long-horizon tasks into manageable steps. Test-time search and training-based methods improve planning but incur high inference costs or require expensive training data. Self-evolving memory instead accumulates reusable experience from agent interaction outcomes into an external memory bank, so planning capability keeps improving at inference time without parameter updates. However, existing self-evolving memory methods share an inherent credit assignment problem: they rely on final task outcomes as feedback, but such outcomes conflate plan quality with execution errors and environmental factors, so the accumulated planning experience is often biased and noisy. To address this problem, we propose Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution (CHIME), a self-evolving memory framework that maintains a separate planning bank and execution bank and follows an attribute-before-memorize principle: CHIME first attributes each task outcome to the plan, the execution, both, or neither, and then updates only the corresponding memory bank. Extensive experiments on four long-horizon agent benchmarks show that CHIME consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-based and self-evolving memory baselines.
